const router = Router();

// Upsert a buyer by name — insert if no existing row with that name, silently ignore duplicate
async function upsertKund(namn: string | null): Promise<void> {
  if (!namn?.trim()) return;
  await db.insert(kunderTable).values({ namn: namn.trim() }).onConflictDoNothing();
}

async function logHistorik(lagerId: number, action: string, andringar?: object) {
  try {
    await db.insert(lagerHistorikTable).values({
      lagerId,
      action,
      andringar: andringar ? JSON.stringify(andringar) : null,
    });
  } catch (_) {}
}

function toNum(val: unknown): number | null {
  if (val === null || val === undefined || val === "") return null;
  const n = Number(val);
  return isNaN(n) ? null : n;
}

function normalizeDim(s: string): string {
  return s.replace(/[xX×]/g, "×").replace(/\s/g, "").replace(",", ".").trim();
}

function calcNomM3(post: typeof lagerPosterTable.$inferSelect): number | null {
  const nomT = post.nomTjocklek ? parseFloat(post.nomTjocklek) : null;
  const nomB = post.nomBredd ? parseFloat(post.nomBredd) : null;
  const lpm = post.lpm ? parseFloat(post.lpm) : null;
  const langdDm = post.langdDm ? parseFloat(post.langdDm) : null;
  const antal = post.antal ?? null;
  // Same logic as computedNomM3() in frontend – round to 3 dp to match table display
  if (nomT && nomB && lpm)
    return parseFloat(((nomT / 1000) * (nomB / 1000) * lpm).toFixed(3));
  if (nomT && nomB && langdDm && antal)
    return parseFloat(((nomT / 1000) * (nomB / 1000) * (langdDm / 10) * antal).toFixed(3));
  return post.m3 ? parseFloat(parseFloat(post.m3).toFixed(3)) : null;
}

// Score how well a prislista row's beskrivning matches the package's profil/varuslag.
// Returns higher score = better match. Penalises beschrivning words not in the package.
function scorePrislistaMatch(
  beskrivning: string | null,
  profil: string | null,
  varuslag: string | null
): number {
  if (!beskrivning) return 0;
  // Strip dimension patterns (e.g. "47×100") and split into words
  const cleaned = beskrivning.toLowerCase().replace(/\d+\s*[x×]\s*\d+/gi, "");
  const descWords = cleaned.split(/\s+/).filter(w => w.length >= 2);
  if (descWords.length === 0) return 0;

  const pkgStr = `${profil ?? ""} ${varuslag ?? ""}`.toLowerCase();
  const pkgWords = new Set(pkgStr.split(/\s+/).filter(w => w.length >= 2));

  let score = 0;
  for (const dw of descWords) {
    if (pkgWords.has(dw)) score += 2;   // exact word hit
    else score -= 1;                     // word in description not in package = penalise
  }
  return score;
}

async function lookupSaljPris(
  specId: number,
  nomTjocklek: number | null,
  nomBredd: number | null,
  nomM3: number | null,
  forcePrislistaId?: number | null,
  profil?: string | null,
  varuslag?: string | null,
  traslag?: string | null,
): Promise<{ saljPris: string | null; saljSum: string | null }> {
  if (!nomTjocklek || !nomBredd || !nomM3) return { saljPris: null, saljSum: null };

  let prislistaId: number | undefined;
  if (forcePrislistaId) {
    prislistaId = forcePrislistaId;
  } else {
    const [spec] = await db
      .select({ kund: specifikationerTable.kund })
      .from(specifikationerTable)
      .where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, specId));
    if (!spec?.kund) return { saljPris: null, saljSum: null };
    const [prislista] = await db
      .select({ id: prislistorTable.id })
      .from(prislistorTable)
      .where(eq(prislistorTable.kund, spec.kund))
      .orderBy(desc(prislistorTable.skapadAt))
      .limit(1);
    if (!prislista) return { saljPris: null, saljSum: null };
    prislistaId = prislista.id;
  }

  const rows = await db
    .select()
    .from(prislistaRaderTable)
    .where(and(eq(prislistaRaderTable.prislistaId, prislistaId), eq(prislistaRaderTable.arRubrik, false)));

  const nomKey = `${nomTjocklek}×${nomBredd}`;
  const dimMatches = rows.filter(r => r.nomMatt && normalizeDim(r.nomMatt) === nomKey);
  if (dimMatches.length === 0) return { saljPris: null, saljSum: null };

  // Pick the row whose beskrivning best matches the package profil/varuslag
  let best = dimMatches[0];
  if (dimMatches.length > 1 && (profil || varuslag || traslag)) {
    // Combine profil + varuslag + traslag so "Imp" (traslag) helps distinguish e.g. Trall vs Klädsel
    const combinedVaruslag = [varuslag, traslag].filter(Boolean).join(" ") || null;
    let bestScore = scorePrislistaMatch(best.beskrivning, profil ?? null, combinedVaruslag);
    for (const row of dimMatches.slice(1)) {
      const s = scorePrislistaMatch(row.beskrivning, profil ?? null, combinedVaruslag);
      if (s > bestScore) { bestScore = s; best = row; }
    }
  }

  if (!best.prisPerM3) return { saljPris: null, saljSum: null };
  const pris = parseFloat(best.prisPerM3);
  const sum = parseFloat((pris * nomM3).toFixed(2));
  return { saljPris: pris.toString(), saljSum: sum.toString() };
}

function mapPost(row: typeof lagerPosterTable.$inferSelect) {
  return {
    ...row,
    nomTjocklek: toNum(row.nomTjocklek),
    nomBredd: toNum(row.nomBredd),
    actTjocklek: toNum(row.actTjocklek),
    actBredd: toNum(row.actBredd),
    langdDm: toNum(row.langdDm),
    m3: toNum(row.m3),
    m3Pris: toNum(row.m3Pris),
    m3Sum: toNum(row.m3Sum),
    avrappM3: toNum(row.avrappM3),
    lpm: toNum(row.lpm),
    actM3: toNum(row.actM3),
    skapadAt: row.skapadAt.toISOString(),
    uppdateradAt: row.uppdateradAt.toISOString(),
  };
}

function parseBody(body: Record<string, unknown>) {
  const kopplatSpecIdRaw = body.kopplatSpecId;
  const kopplatSpecId = kopplatSpecIdRaw !== null && kopplatSpecIdRaw !== undefined && kopplatSpecIdRaw !== ""
    ? parseInt(kopplatSpecIdRaw as string)
    : null;
  const ntrByPaket: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  if (Array.isArray(body.ntrByPaket)) {
    for (const row of body.ntrByPaket) {
      if (typeof row !== "object" || row === null) continue;
      const value = row as Record<string, unknown>;
      const lagerPostId = Number(value.lagerPostId);
      if (Number.isInteger(lagerPostId) && lagerPostId > 0) {
        ntrByPaket[String(lagerPostId)] = value.ntrKlass;
      }
    }
  } else if (typeof body.ntrByPaket === "object" && body.ntrByPaket !== null) {
    Object.assign(ntrByPaket, body.ntrByPaket);
  }
  return {
    typ: typeof body.typ === "string" ? body.typ : "leverans",
    kund: typeof body.kund === "string" ? body.kund : null,
    adress: typeof body.adress === "string" ? body.adress : null,
    trailerNr: typeof body.trailerNr === "string" ? body.trailerNr : null,
    orderNr: typeof body.orderNr === "string" ? body.orderNr : null,
    erReferens: typeof body.erReferens === "string" ? body.erReferens : null,
    anteckning: typeof body.anteckning === "string" ? body.anteckning : null,
    kopplatSpecId: kopplatSpecId && !isNaN(kopplatSpecId) ? kopplatSpecId : null,
    paketIds: Array.isArray(body.paketIds)
      ? [...new Set((body.paketIds as unknown[]).map(Number).filter(n => Number.isInteger(n) && n > 0))]
      : [],
    certifikat: Array.isArray(body.certifikat) ? (body.certifikat as unknown[]).filter((s): s is string => typeof s === "string") : [],
    showCertificationOnDocument: body.showCertificationOnDocument === true,
    behandlingTyp: typeof body.behandlingTyp === "string" ? body.behandlingTyp.trim().toUpperCase() : null,
    utforare: typeof body.utforare === "string" ? body.utforare : null,
    ntrKlass: typeof body.ntrKlass === "string" ? body.ntrKlass.trim().toUpperCase() : null,
    olikaNtrPerPaket: typeof body.olikaNtrPerPaket === "boolean" ? body.olikaNtrPerPaket : false,
    kulor: typeof body.kulor === "string" ? body.kulor : null,
    ncsRalKod: typeof body.ncsRalKod === "string" ? body.ncsRalKod : null,
    antalStrykningar: body.antalStrykningar === null || body.antalStrykningar === "" ? null : Number(body.antalStrykningar),
    maladeSidor: body.maladeSidor === null || body.maladeSidor === "" ? null : Number(body.maladeSidor),
    behandlingsInstruktion: typeof body.behandlingsInstruktion === "string" ? body.behandlingsInstruktion : null,
    ntrByPaket,
    applyNtrKlass: typeof body.applyNtrKlass === "string" ? body.applyNtrKlass.trim().toUpperCase() : null,
  };
}

const NTR_KLASSER = new Set(["NTR_A", "NTR_AB", "EJ_ANGIVEN"]);
const BEHANDLINGSTYPER = new Set(["IMPREGNERING", "GRUNDMALNING"]);
function blank(value: string | null | undefined) { return !value?.trim(); }
function behandlingTypFor(spec: { typ: string; behandlingTyp: string | null }) {
  return spec.behandlingTyp ?? (spec.typ === "impregnering" ? "IMPREGNERING" : null);
}
function isBehandling(spec: { typ: string; behandlingTyp: string | null }) {
  return behandlingTypFor(spec) !== null;
}
function mapSpec<T extends { typ: string; behandlingTyp: string | null }>(spec: T): T & { behandlingTyp: string | null } {
  return { ...spec, behandlingTyp: behandlingTypFor(spec) };
}
async function mapSpecWithSender<T extends { typ: string; behandlingTyp: string | null }>(spec: T) {
  const [branding] = await db.select().from(brandingSettingsTable).where(eq(brandingSettingsTable.id, 1)).limit(1);
  // The startup migration creates this row; this guard keeps legacy response handling safe.
  if (!branding) return { ...mapSpec(spec), sender: null, documentSenderSnapshot: null };
  const documentSenderSnapshot = profileResponse(senderSnapshotProfile(spec, brandingToDocumentProfile(branding)));
  return {
    ...mapSpec(spec),
    showCertificationOnDocument: hasSenderSnapshot(spec)
      ? spec.senderShowCertificationOnDocument
      : spec.showCertificationOnDocument,
    sender: documentSenderSnapshot,
    documentSenderSnapshot,
  };
}

async function captureSpecSenderSnapshot(spec: typeof specifikationerTable.$inferSelect) {
  const [branding] = await db.select().from(brandingSettingsTable).where(eq(brandingSettingsTable.id, 1)).limit(1);
  if (!branding) return;
  const baseValues = senderSnapshotForCapture(spec, brandingToDocumentProfile(branding));
  const values = baseValues && { ...baseValues, senderShowCertificationOnDocument: spec.showCertificationOnDocument };
  if (!values) return;
  await db.update(specifikationerTable)
    .set(values)
    .where(and(eq(specifikationerTable.id, spec.id), isNull(specifikationerTable.senderCompanyName)));
}
function treatmentError(body: ReturnType<typeof parseBody>, confirming = false): string | null {
  if (body.behandlingTyp !== null && !BEHANDLINGSTYPER.has(body.behandlingTyp)) return "Ogiltig behandlingstyp";
  if (body.ntrKlass !== null && !NTR_KLASSER.has(body.ntrKlass)) return "Ogiltig NTR-klass";
  if (body.applyNtrKlass !== null && !NTR_KLASSER.has(body.applyNtrKlass)) return "Ogiltig NTR-klass";
  if (body.behandlingTyp === "IMPREGNERING" && confirming && !["NTR_A", "NTR_AB"].includes(body.ntrKlass ?? "")) return "Impregnering måste ha NTR-A eller NTR-AB vid bekräftelse";
  if (body.behandlingTyp === "GRUNDMALNING") {
    if (blank(body.kulor)) return "Grundmålning kräver kulör";
    if (body.ntrKlass || body.applyNtrKlass) return "Grundmålning får inte ha NTR-data";
    if (body.antalStrykningar !== null && (!Number.isInteger(body.antalStrykningar) || body.antalStrykningar <= 0)) return "Antal strykningar måste vara ett positivt heltal";
    if (body.maladeSidor !== null && (!Number.isInteger(body.maladeSidor) || body.maladeSidor < 1 || body.maladeSidor > 4)) return "Målade sidor måste vara mellan 1 och 4";
  }
  return null;
}

// GET /api/spec – list all specs (optional ?typ=leverans|hyvling|impregnering)
router.get("/", async (req, res) => {
  const typFilter = typeof req.query.typ === "string" ? req.query.typ : null;
  const s = specifikationerTable;
  const query = db
    .select({
      id: s.id,
      typ: s.typ,
      hyvlingNr: s.hyvlingNr,
      specNr: s.specNr,
      kund: s.kund,
      behandlingTyp: s.behandlingTyp,
      utforare: s.utforare,
      ntrKlass: s.ntrKlass,
      olikaNtrPerPaket: s.olikaNtrPerPaket,
      kulor: s.kulor,
      ncsRalKod: s.ncsRalKod,
      antalStrykningar: s.antalStrykningar,
      maladeSidor: s.maladeSidor,
      behandlingsInstruktion: s.behandlingsInstruktion,
      adress: s.adress,
      trailerNr: s.trailerNr,
      orderNr: s.orderNr,
      erReferens: s.erReferens,
      anteckning: s.anteckning,
      status: s.status,
      bekraftadAt: s.bekraftadAt,
      skapadAt: s.skapadAt,
      senderCompanyName: s.senderCompanyName,
      senderLogoPath: s.senderLogoPath,
      senderStreetAddress: s.senderStreetAddress,
      senderPostalCode: s.senderPostalCode,
      senderCity: s.senderCity,
      senderCountry: s.senderCountry,
      senderPhone: s.senderPhone,
      senderEmail: s.senderEmail,
      senderOrganizationNumber: s.senderOrganizationNumber,
      senderVatNumber: s.senderVatNumber,
      senderWebsite: s.senderWebsite,
      senderCertificationText: s.senderCertificationText,
      senderCertificationNumber: s.senderCertificationNumber,
      senderInvoiceDetails: s.senderInvoiceDetails,
      kopplatSpecId: s.kopplatSpecId,
      antalPaket: sql<number>`COUNT(${specPaketTable.id})::int`,
      totalM3: sql<number>`ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(${lagerPosterTable.m3}::numeric), 0), 2)`,
      totalActM3: sql<number>`ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(
        COALESCE(
          ${lagerPosterTable.actM3}::numeric,
          (${lagerPosterTable.actTjocklek}::numeric / 1000)
            * (${lagerPosterTable.actBredd}::numeric / 1000)
            * ${lagerPosterTable.lpm}::numeric
        )
      ), 0), 2)`,
    })
    .from(s)
    .leftJoin(specPaketTable, eq(specPaketTable.specId, s.id))
    .leftJoin(lagerPosterTable, eq(specPaketTable.lagerPostId, lagerPosterTable.id))
    .groupBy(s.id)
    .orderBy(desc(s.skapadAt));
  const rows = typFilter
    ? await query.where(eq(s.typ, typFilter))
    : await query;
  res.json(await Promise.all(rows.map(mapSpecWithSender)));
});

// POST /api/spec/scan-import – skapa spec direkt från AI-skannat dokument
// Paket sparas som ordinarie aktuellt lager och länkas till den nya specan via
// spec_paket. Importursprunget sparas i lagerhistoriken, inte som avrappTyp,
// så paketen syns i lagerkarta, områdesstatistik och inventering.
router.post("/scan-import", async (req, res) => {
  const { avsandare, datum, paket } = req.body as {
    avsandare: string | null;
    datum: string | null;
    paket: Array<{
      pktNr?: string | null;
      traslag?: string | null;
      profil?: string | null;
      kvalitet?: string | null;
      behandling?: string | null;
      utforande?: string | null;
      omradeId?: number | null;
      fackId?: number | null;
      nomTjocklek?: number | null;
      nomBredd?: number | null;
      aktTjocklek?: number | null;
      aktBredd?: number | null;
      langdDm?: number | null;
      antal?: number | null;
      m3?: number | null;
      m3Pris?: number | null;
      m3Sum?: number | null;
      mottaget?: string | null;
      datumIn?: string | null;
      anteckning?: string | null;
    }>;
  };

  if (!Array.isArray(paket) || paket.length === 0) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: "Inga paket angivna" });
  }

  const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const datumIn = datum || today;
  const kund = avsandare?.trim() || null;

  const resolvedPlaceringar = await Promise.all(
    paket.map((p) => resolveStructuredPlatsPatch({
      omradeId: p.omradeId ?? null,
      fackId: p.fackId ?? null,
    })),
  );
  const invalidPlacering = resolvedPlaceringar.find((result) => !result.ok);
  if (invalidPlacering && !invalidPlacering.ok) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: invalidPlacering.error });
  }

  const GRAN_REGEL_NT = new Set([45, 47]);
  const GRAN_REGEL_NB = new Set([45, 50, 70, 75, 95, 100, 120, 125]);
  const result = await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
    const [maxSpecNrRow] = await tx
      .select({ maxNr: sql<number>`COALESCE(MAX(${specifikationerTable.specNr}), 0)` })
      .from(specifikationerTable);
    const newSpecNr = (maxSpecNrRow?.maxNr ?? 0) + 1;
    const [spec] = await tx
      .insert(specifikationerTable)
      .values({ typ: "leverans", kund, status: "utkast", specNr: newSpecNr })
      .returning();

    const insertedIds: number[] = [];
    for (const [index, p] of paket.entries()) {
      const tsLow = (p.traslag ?? "").toLowerCase();
      const profil = (
        tsLow.startsWith("gran") &&
        p.nomTjocklek != null && GRAN_REGEL_NT.has(p.nomTjocklek) &&
        p.nomBredd != null && GRAN_REGEL_NB.has(p.nomBredd) &&
        p.langdDm === 25
      ) ? "Regel" : p.profil ?? null;

      let m3 = p.m3 ?? null;
      if (!m3 && p.langdDm && p.antal) {
        const tk = p.aktTjocklek ?? p.nomTjocklek;
        const br = p.aktBredd ?? p.nomBredd;
        if (tk && br) {
          m3 = parseFloat(((tk / 1000) * (br / 1000) * (p.langdDm / 10) * p.antal).toFixed(4));
        }
      }

      const placering = resolvedPlaceringar[index];
      if (!placering.ok) throw new Error(placering.error);
      const [inserted] = await tx
        .insert(lagerPosterTable)
        .values({
          pktNr: p.pktNr ?? null,
          traslag: p.traslag ?? null,
          profil,
          kvalitet: p.kvalitet ?? null,
          behandling: normaliseraPaketBehandling(p.behandling),
          utforande: normaliseraPaketUtforande(p.utforande),
          nomTjocklek: p.nomTjocklek?.toString() ?? null,
          nomBredd: p.nomBredd?.toString() ?? null,
          actTjocklek: p.aktTjocklek?.toString() ?? null,
          actBredd: p.aktBredd?.toString() ?? null,
          langdDm: p.langdDm?.toString() ?? null,
          antal: p.antal ?? null,
          m3: m3?.toString() ?? null,
          m3Pris: p.m3Pris?.toString() ?? null,
          m3Sum: p.m3Sum?.toString() ?? null,
          lpm: p.langdDm && p.antal ? ((p.langdDm / 10) * p.antal).toFixed(2) : null,
          mottaget: p.mottaget ?? avsandare ?? null,
          datumIn,
          anteckning: p.anteckning ?? null,
          leverantor: avsandare ?? null,
          avrappTyp: null,
          plats: placering.patch.plats as string | null,
          omradeId: placering.patch.omradeId as number | null,
          fackId: placering.patch.fackId as number | null,
        })
        .returning({ id: lagerPosterTable.id });
      insertedIds.push(inserted.id);
    }

    await tx.insert(specPaketTable).values(
      insertedIds.map((id) => ({ specId: spec.id, lagerPostId: id })),
    );
    await tx.insert(lagerHistorikTable).values(
      insertedIds.map((id, index) => ({
        lagerId: id,
        action: "create",
        changedBy: (req as typeof req & { userId?: string }).userId ?? null,
        andringar: JSON.stringify({
          source: "spec_import",
          omradeId: resolvedPlaceringar[index].ok ? resolvedPlaceringar[index].patch.omradeId : null,
          fackId: resolvedPlaceringar[index].ok ? resolvedPlaceringar[index].patch.fackId : null,
        }),
      })),
    );

    return { specId: spec.id, antalPaket: insertedIds.length };
  });

  res.json(result);
});

// POST /api/spec – create new spec
router.post("/", async (req, res) => {
  const body = parseBody(req.body as Record<string, unknown>);
  if (body.typ === "behandling") body.typ = "impregnering";
  if (!["leverans", "hyvling", "impregnering"].includes(body.typ)) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Ogiltig specifikationstyp" });
  if (body.typ === "impregnering" && !body.behandlingTyp) body.behandlingTyp = "IMPREGNERING";
  const validationError = treatmentError(body);
  if (validationError) return res.status(400).json({ error: validationError });

  let hyvlingNr: number | null = null;
  if (body.typ === "hyvling") {
    const [maxRow] = await db
      .select({ maxNr: sql<number>`COALESCE(MAX(${specifikationerTable.hyvlingNr}), 0)` })
      .from(specifikationerTable)
      .where(eq(specifikationerTable.typ, "hyvling"));
    hyvlingNr = (maxRow?.maxNr ?? 0) + 1;
  }

  let specNr: number | null = null;
  if (body.typ === "leverans") {
    const [maxRow] = await db
      .select({ maxNr: sql<number>`COALESCE(MAX(${specifikationerTable.specNr}), 0)` })
      .from(specifikationerTable);
    specNr = (maxRow?.maxNr ?? 0) + 1;
  }

  const [spec] = await db
    .insert(specifikationerTable)
    .values({ typ: body.typ, hyvlingNr, specNr, kund: body.kund ?? null, adress: body.adress ?? null, trailerNr: body.trailerNr ?? null, orderNr: body.orderNr ?? null, erReferens: body.erReferens ?? null, anteckning: body.anteckning ?? null, kopplatSpecId: body.kopplatSpecId ?? null, certifikat: body.certifikat, showCertificationOnDocument: body.showCertificationOnDocument,
      behandlingTyp: body.behandlingTyp, utforare: body.utforare, ntrKlass: body.behandlingTyp === "GRUNDMALNING" ? null : body.ntrKlass,
      olikaNtrPerPaket: body.behandlingTyp === "GRUNDMALNING" ? false : body.olikaNtrPerPaket,
      kulor: body.behandlingTyp === "GRUNDMALNING" ? body.kulor : null, ncsRalKod: body.behandlingTyp === "GRUNDMALNING" ? body.ncsRalKod : null,
      antalStrykningar: body.behandlingTyp === "GRUNDMALNING" ? body.antalStrykningar : null, maladeSidor: body.behandlingTyp === "GRUNDMALNING" ? body.maladeSidor : null,
      behandlingsInstruktion: body.behandlingTyp === "GRUNDMALNING" ? body.behandlingsInstruktion : null })
    .returning();
  if (body.paketIds.length > 0) {
    const rows = body.paketIds.map(lagerPostId => {
      const requestedNtr = body.ntrByPaket[String(lagerPostId)];
      const rowNtr = body.behandlingTyp !== "IMPREGNERING"
        ? null
        : body.olikaNtrPerPaket && typeof requestedNtr === "string"
          ? requestedNtr.trim().toUpperCase()
          : body.ntrKlass;
      if (rowNtr !== null && !NTR_KLASSER.has(rowNtr)) {
        throw new Error("Ogiltig NTR-klass per paket");
      }
      return { specId: spec.id, lagerPostId, ntrKlass: rowNtr };
    });
    await db.insert(specPaketTable).values(rows);
  }
  return res.json(await mapSpecWithSender(spec));
});

// GET /api/spec/by-lager/:lagerId – which specs contain this lager post?
router.get("/by-lager/:lagerId", async (req, res) => {
  const lagerId = parseInt(req.params.lagerId);
  if (isNaN(lagerId)) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Ogiltigt id" });

  const rows = await db
    .select({
      id: specifikationerTable.id,
      typ: specifikationerTable.typ,
      hyvlingNr: specifikationerTable.hyvlingNr,
      specNr: specifikationerTable.specNr,
      kund: specifikationerTable.kund,
      behandlingTyp: specifikationerTable.behandlingTyp,
      utforare: specifikationerTable.utforare,
      ntrKlass: specifikationerTable.ntrKlass,
      olikaNtrPerPaket: specifikationerTable.olikaNtrPerPaket,
      kulor: specifikationerTable.kulor,
      ncsRalKod: specifikationerTable.ncsRalKod,
      antalStrykningar: specifikationerTable.antalStrykningar,
      maladeSidor: specifikationerTable.maladeSidor,
      behandlingsInstruktion: specifikationerTable.behandlingsInstruktion,
      adress: specifikationerTable.adress,
      trailerNr: specifikationerTable.trailerNr,
      orderNr: specifikationerTable.orderNr,
      erReferens: specifikationerTable.erReferens,
      anteckning: specifikationerTable.anteckning,
      status: specifikationerTable.status,
      bekraftadAt: specifikationerTable.bekraftadAt,
      skapadAt: specifikationerTable.skapadAt,
      senderCompanyName: specifikationerTable.senderCompanyName,
      senderLogoPath: specifikationerTable.senderLogoPath,
      senderStreetAddress: specifikationerTable.senderStreetAddress,
      senderPostalCode: specifikationerTable.senderPostalCode,
      senderCity: specifikationerTable.senderCity,
      senderCountry: specifikationerTable.senderCountry,
      senderPhone: specifikationerTable.senderPhone,
      senderEmail: specifikationerTable.senderEmail,
      senderOrganizationNumber: specifikationerTable.senderOrganizationNumber,
      senderVatNumber: specifikationerTable.senderVatNumber,
      senderWebsite: specifikationerTable.senderWebsite,
      senderCertificationText: specifikationerTable.senderCertificationText,
      senderCertificationNumber: specifikationerTable.senderCertificationNumber,
      senderInvoiceDetails: specifikationerTable.senderInvoiceDetails,
    })
    .from(specPaketTable)
    .innerJoin(specifikationerTable, eq(specifikationerTable.id, specPaketTable.specId))
    .where(eq(specPaketTable.lagerPostId, lagerId));

  res.json(await Promise.all(rows.map(mapSpecWithSender)));
});

// GET /api/spec/:id – get spec with packages
router.get("/:id", async (req, res) => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
  if (isNaN(id)) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Ogiltigt id" });

  const [spec] = await db.select().from(specifikationerTable).where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, id));
  if (!spec) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Spec ej hittad" });

  const paketRows = await db
    .select({
      post: lagerPosterTable,
      saljPris: specPaketTable.saljPris,
      saljSum: specPaketTable.saljSum,
      ntrKlass: specPaketTable.ntrKlass,
    })
    .from(specPaketTable)
    .innerJoin(lagerPosterTable, eq(specPaketTable.lagerPostId, lagerPosterTable.id))
    .where(eq(specPaketTable.specId, id));

  // Fetch linked spec info if exists
  let kopplatSpec: { id: number; typ: string; hyvlingNr: number | null; specNr: number | null; kund: string | null; status: string } | null = null;
  if (spec.kopplatSpecId) {
    const [linked] = await db
      .select({ id: specifikationerTable.id, typ: specifikationerTable.typ, hyvlingNr: specifikationerTable.hyvlingNr, specNr: specifikationerTable.specNr, kund: specifikationerTable.kund, status: specifikationerTable.status })
      .from(specifikationerTable)
      .where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, spec.kopplatSpecId));
    kopplatSpec = linked ?? null;
  }

  // Fetch any specs that link back to this spec (reverse links)
  const reverseLinks = await db
    .select({ id: specifikationerTable.id, typ: specifikationerTable.typ, hyvlingNr: specifikationerTable.hyvlingNr, specNr: specifikationerTable.specNr, kund: specifikationerTable.kund, status: specifikationerTable.status })
    .from(specifikationerTable)
    .where(eq(specifikationerTable.kopplatSpecId, id));

  res.json({
    ...(await mapSpecWithSender(spec)),
    paket: paketRows.map(r => ({
      ...mapPost(r.post),
      saljPris: toNum(r.saljPris),
      saljSum: toNum(r.saljSum),
      ntrKlass: r.ntrKlass,
    })),
    kopplatSpec,
    reverseLinks,
  });
});

// GET /api/spec/kollision?lagerIds=1,2,3[&excludeSpecId=4] – check if any lager posts already belong to other specs
router.get("/kollision", async (req, res) => {
  const raw = typeof req.query.lagerIds === "string" ? req.query.lagerIds : "";
  const excludeSpecId = req.query.excludeSpecId ? parseInt(req.query.excludeSpecId as string) : null;
  const ids = raw.split(",").map(Number).filter(n => !isNaN(n) && n > 0);
  if (ids.length === 0) return res.json({ kollisioner: [] });

  const baseWhere = inArray(specPaketTable.lagerPostId, ids);
  const rows = await db
    .select({
      lagerId: specPaketTable.lagerPostId,
      specId: specPaketTable.specId,
      kund: specifikationerTable.kund,
      status: specifikationerTable.status,
    })
    .from(specPaketTable)
    .innerJoin(specifikationerTable, eq(specPaketTable.specId, specifikationerTable.id))
    .where(
      excludeSpecId
        ? and(baseWhere, ne(specPaketTable.specId, excludeSpecId))
        : baseWhere
    );

  res.json({ kollisioner: rows });
});

// POST /api/spec/:id/paket – add a single package to an existing spec
router.post("/:id/paket", async (req, res) => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
  if (isNaN(id)) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Ogiltigt id" });
  const lagerId = parseInt(req.body?.lagerId);
  if (isNaN(lagerId)) return res.status(400).json({ error: "lagerId saknas" });

  const [spec] = await db.select().from(specifikationerTable).where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, id));
  if (!spec) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Spec ej hittad" });
  // Check if already in spec (avoid duplicates)
  const existing = await db
    .select()
    .from(specPaketTable)
    .where(and(eq(specPaketTable.specId, id), eq(specPaketTable.lagerPostId, lagerId)));

  if (existing.length === 0) {
    const [pkg] = await db.select().from(lagerPosterTable).where(eq(lagerPosterTable.id, lagerId));
    const nomM3 = pkg ? calcNomM3(pkg) : null;
    const { saljPris, saljSum } = await lookupSaljPris(id, toNum(pkg?.nomTjocklek), toNum(pkg?.nomBredd), nomM3, null, pkg?.profil, pkg?.varuslag, pkg?.traslag);
    await db.insert(specPaketTable).values({ specId: id, lagerPostId: lagerId, saljPris, saljSum, ntrKlass: behandlingTypFor(spec) === "IMPREGNERING" ? spec.ntrKlass : null });
  }

  res.json({ ok: true, already: existing.length > 0 });
});

// POST /api/spec/:id/paket/bulk – add multiple packages to an existing spec (skip duplicates)
router.post("/:id/paket/bulk", async (req, res) => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
  if (isNaN(id)) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Ogiltigt id" });
  const ids: number[] = Array.isArray(req.body?.lagerIds)
    ? (req.body.lagerIds as unknown[]).map(Number).filter(n => !isNaN(n))
    : [];
  if (ids.length === 0) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Inga lagerIds" });
  const [spec] = await db.select().from(specifikationerTable).where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, id));
  if (!spec) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Spec ej hittad" });

  const existing = await db
    .select({ lagerPostId: specPaketTable.lagerPostId })
    .from(specPaketTable)
    .where(eq(specPaketTable.specId, id));
  const existingSet = new Set(existing.map(r => r.lagerPostId));
  const toInsert = ids.filter(lid => !existingSet.has(lid));

  if (toInsert.length > 0) {
    const pkgs = toInsert.length > 0
      ? await db.select().from(lagerPosterTable).where(inArray(lagerPosterTable.id, toInsert))
      : [];
    const pkgMap = new Map(pkgs.map(p => [p.id, p]));
    const rows = await Promise.all(toInsert.map(async lagerPostId => {
      const pkg = pkgMap.get(lagerPostId);
      const nomM3 = pkg ? calcNomM3(pkg) : null;
      const { saljPris, saljSum } = await lookupSaljPris(id, toNum(pkg?.nomTjocklek), toNum(pkg?.nomBredd), nomM3, null, pkg?.profil, pkg?.varuslag, pkg?.traslag);
      return { specId: id, lagerPostId, saljPris, saljSum, ntrKlass: behandlingTypFor(spec) === "IMPREGNERING" ? spec.ntrKlass : null };
    }));
    await db.insert(specPaketTable).values(rows);
  }
  res.json({ ok: true, tillagda: toInsert.length, duplicates: ids.length - toInsert.length });
});

// PATCH /api/spec/:id/paket/:lagerId/pris – manually set salj-price for one package
router.patch("/:id/paket/:lagerId/pris", async (req, res) => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
  const lagerId = parseInt(req.params.lagerId);
  if (isNaN(id) || isNaN(lagerId)) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Ogiltigt id" });

  const rawPris = req.body?.saljPris;
  const saljPrisNum = rawPris === null || rawPris === undefined || rawPris === "" ? null : parseFloat(rawPris);
  if (saljPrisNum !== null && isNaN(saljPrisNum)) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Ogiltigt pris" });

  let saljPris: string | null = null;
  let saljSum: string | null = null;

  if (saljPrisNum !== null) {
    const [pkg] = await db.select().from(lagerPosterTable).where(eq(lagerPosterTable.id, lagerId));
    const nomM3 = pkg ? calcNomM3(pkg) : null;
    saljPris = saljPrisNum.toString();
    saljSum = nomM3 != null ? (saljPrisNum * nomM3).toFixed(2) : null;
  }

  await db
    .update(specPaketTable)
    .set({ saljPris, saljSum })
    .where(and(eq(specPaketTable.specId, id), eq(specPaketTable.lagerPostId, lagerId)));

  res.json({ ok: true, saljPris: saljPris != null ? parseFloat(saljPris) : null, saljSum: saljSum != null ? parseFloat(saljSum) : null });
});

// DELETE /api/spec/:id/paket/:lagerId – remove a single package from spec
router.delete("/:id/paket/:lagerId", async (req, res) => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
  const lagerId = parseInt(req.params.lagerId);
  if (isNaN(id) || isNaN(lagerId)) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Ogiltigt id" });

  await db
    .delete(specPaketTable)
    .where(and(eq(specPaketTable.specId, id), eq(specPaketTable.lagerPostId, lagerId)));

  res.json({ ok: true });
});

// PUT /api/spec/:id – update spec (header + replace packages)
router.put("/:id", async (req, res) => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
  if (isNaN(id)) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Ogiltigt id" });

  const rawBody = req.body as Record<string, unknown>;
  const body = parseBody(rawBody);
  const [current] = await db.select().from(specifikationerTable).where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, id));
  if (!current) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Spec ej hittad" });
  const currentTreatment = behandlingTypFor(current);
  if (!body.behandlingTyp && currentTreatment) body.behandlingTyp = currentTreatment;
  // Older clients do not send the additive fields on every PUT. Preserve
  // them unless the caller explicitly supplies a replacement value.
  if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rawBody, "utforare")) body.utforare = current.utforare;
  if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rawBody, "kulor")) body.kulor = current.kulor;
  if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rawBody, "ncsRalKod")) body.ncsRalKod = current.ncsRalKod;
  if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rawBody, "antalStrykningar")) body.antalStrykningar = current.antalStrykningar;
  if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rawBody, "maladeSidor")) body.maladeSidor = current.maladeSidor;
  if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rawBody, "behandlingsInstruktion")) body.behandlingsInstruktion = current.behandlingsInstruktion;
  if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rawBody, "olikaNtrPerPaket")) body.olikaNtrPerPaket = current.olikaNtrPerPaket;
  if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rawBody, "erReferens")) body.erReferens = current.erReferens;
  if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rawBody, "anteckning")) body.anteckning = current.anteckning;
  if (body.typ === "behandling") body.typ = "impregnering";
  const requestedTreatment = body.behandlingTyp;
  if (requestedTreatment && body.typ !== "impregnering") body.typ = "impregnering";
  if (current.status === "bekraftad" || current.status === "returnerad") {
    if (requestedTreatment !== currentTreatment || body.typ !== current.typ) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: "Behandlingstyp kan inte ändras på bekräftad eller returnerad spec" });
    }
  }
  if (!body.ntrKlass && currentTreatment === "IMPREGNERING" && !Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rawBody, "ntrKlass")) body.ntrKlass = current.ntrKlass;
  const validationError = treatmentError(body);
  if (validationError) return res.status(400).json({ error: validationError });
  if (body.behandlingTyp === "GRUNDMALNING") body.ntrKlass = null;

  // Preserve existing salj-prices before deleting
  const existingPrices = await db
    .select({ lagerPostId: specPaketTable.lagerPostId, saljPris: specPaketTable.saljPris, saljSum: specPaketTable.saljSum })
    .from(specPaketTable)
    .where(eq(specPaketTable.specId, id));
  const priceMap = new Map(existingPrices.map(r => [r.lagerPostId, { saljPris: r.saljPris, saljSum: r.saljSum }]));
  const existingNtr = await db
    .select({ lagerPostId: specPaketTable.lagerPostId, ntrKlass: specPaketTable.ntrKlass })
    .from(specPaketTable).where(eq(specPaketTable.specId, id));
  const ntrMap = new Map(existingNtr.map(r => [r.lagerPostId, r.ntrKlass]));
  const explicitNtr = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rawBody, "ntrKlass") || !!body.applyNtrKlass;
  const requestedDifferent = body.behandlingTyp === "IMPREGNERING" ? body.olikaNtrPerPaket : false;
  const candidateNtr = body.paketIds.map((lagerId) => {
    const requested = body.ntrByPaket[String(lagerId)];
    return typeof requested === "string" ? requested.trim().toUpperCase()
      : (requestedDifferent ? body.ntrKlass : (ntrMap.get(lagerId) ?? body.ntrKlass));
  });
  if (candidateNtr.some((ntr) => ntr !== null && !NTR_KLASSER.has(ntr))) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Ogiltig NTR-klass per paket" });
  const mixed = candidateNtr.includes("NTR_A") && candidateNtr.includes("NTR_AB");
  if (body.behandlingTyp === "IMPREGNERING" && !requestedDifferent && mixed && !explicitNtr) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: "Blandade NTR-klasser kräver NTR-klass eller applyNtrKlass när olika per paket stängs av" });
  }
  const applyNtr = body.applyNtrKlass ?? body.ntrKlass;

  await db
    .update(specifikationerTable)
    .set({ typ: body.typ, kund: body.kund ?? null, adress: body.adress ?? null, trailerNr: body.trailerNr ?? null, orderNr: body.orderNr ?? null, erReferens: body.erReferens ?? null, anteckning: body.anteckning ?? null, kopplatSpecId: body.kopplatSpecId ?? null, certifikat: body.certifikat, showCertificationOnDocument: body.showCertificationOnDocument,
      behandlingTyp: body.behandlingTyp, utforare: body.utforare, ntrKlass: body.behandlingTyp === "IMPREGNERING" ? body.ntrKlass : null,
      olikaNtrPerPaket: requestedDifferent, kulor: body.behandlingTyp === "GRUNDMALNING" ? body.kulor : null, ncsRalKod: body.behandlingTyp === "GRUNDMALNING" ? body.ncsRalKod : null,
      antalStrykningar: body.behandlingTyp === "GRUNDMALNING" ? body.antalStrykningar : null, maladeSidor: body.behandlingTyp === "GRUNDMALNING" ? body.maladeSidor : null,
      behandlingsInstruktion: body.behandlingTyp === "GRUNDMALNING" ? body.behandlingsInstruktion : null, uppdateradAt: new Date() })
    .where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, id));

  await db.delete(specPaketTable).where(eq(specPaketTable.specId, id));
  if (body.paketIds.length > 0) {
    const rows = await Promise.all(body.paketIds.map(async lagerPostId => {
      const existing = priceMap.get(lagerPostId);
      const requestedNtr = body.ntrByPaket[String(lagerPostId)];
      const rowNtr = body.behandlingTyp !== "IMPREGNERING"
        ? null
        : !requestedDifferent
          ? applyNtr
          : typeof requestedNtr === "string"
            ? requestedNtr.trim().toUpperCase()
            : (ntrMap.get(lagerPostId) ?? body.ntrKlass);
      if (rowNtr !== null && !NTR_KLASSER.has(rowNtr)) throw new Error("Ogiltig NTR-klass per paket");
      if (existing) return { specId: id, lagerPostId, saljPris: existing.saljPris, saljSum: existing.saljSum, ntrKlass: rowNtr };
      // New package — look up price
      const [pkg] = await db.select().from(lagerPosterTable).where(eq(lagerPosterTable.id, lagerPostId));
      const nomM3 = pkg ? calcNomM3(pkg) : null;
      const { saljPris, saljSum } = await lookupSaljPris(id, toNum(pkg?.nomTjocklek), toNum(pkg?.nomBredd), nomM3, null, pkg?.profil, pkg?.varuslag, pkg?.traslag);
      return { specId: id, lagerPostId, saljPris, saljSum, ntrKlass: rowNtr };
    }));
    await db.insert(specPaketTable).values(rows);
  }

  const [spec] = await db.select().from(specifikationerTable).where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, id));
  res.json(await mapSpecWithSender(spec));
});

// POST /api/spec/:id/tillamp-prislista – recalculate salj-prices for all packages based on chosen prislista
router.post("/:id/tillamp-prislista", async (req, res) => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
  if (isNaN(id)) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Ogiltigt id" });

  const bodyPrislistaId = req.body?.prislistaId ? parseInt(req.body.prislistaId) : null;
  const forcePrislistaId = bodyPrislistaId && !isNaN(bodyPrislistaId) ? bodyPrislistaId : null;

  // Validate the prislista exists if provided
  if (forcePrislistaId) {
    const [check] = await db.select({ id: prislistorTable.id }).from(prislistorTable).where(eq(prislistorTable.id, forcePrislistaId));
    if (!check) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Prislista hittades inte" });
  }

  const paketRows = await db
    .select({ lagerPostId: specPaketTable.lagerPostId, post: lagerPosterTable })
    .from(specPaketTable)
    .innerJoin(lagerPosterTable, eq(specPaketTable.lagerPostId, lagerPosterTable.id))
    .where(eq(specPaketTable.specId, id));

  let uppdaterade = 0;
  let matchade = 0;
  for (const { lagerPostId, post } of paketRows) {
    const nomM3 = calcNomM3(post);
    const { saljPris, saljSum } = await lookupSaljPris(id, toNum(post.nomTjocklek), toNum(post.nomBredd), nomM3, forcePrislistaId, post.profil, post.varuslag, post.traslag);
    await db
      .update(specPaketTable)
      .set({ saljPris, saljSum })
      .where(and(eq(specPaketTable.specId, id), eq(specPaketTable.lagerPostId, lagerPostId)));
    uppdaterade++;
    if (saljPris != null) matchade++;
  }

  res.json({ ok: true, uppdaterade, matchade, ej_matchade: uppdaterade - matchade });
});

// POST /api/spec/:id/konvertera-till-hyvling – convert leverans spec → hyvling
router.post("/:id/konvertera-till-hyvling", async (req, res) => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
  if (isNaN(id)) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Ogiltigt id" });

  const [spec] = await db.select().from(specifikationerTable).where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, id));
  if (!spec) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Spec ej hittad" });
  if (spec.typ === "hyvling") return res.status(400).json({ error: "Redan en hyvlingsorder" });
  if (spec.status === "bekraftad") return res.status(400).json({ error: "Bekräftade specar kan inte konverteras — återöppna först" });

  // Tilldela nästa hyvlingsnummer
  const [maxRow] = await db
    .select({ maxNr: sql<number>`COALESCE(MAX(${specifikationerTable.hyvlingNr}), 0)` })
    .from(specifikationerTable)
    .where(eq(specifikationerTable.typ, "hyvling"));
  const hyvlingNr = (maxRow?.maxNr ?? 0) + 1;

  await db
    .update(specifikationerTable)
    .set({ typ: "hyvling", hyvlingNr, uppdateradAt: new Date() })
    .where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, id));

  const [updated] = await db.select().from(specifikationerTable).where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, id));
  res.json(updated);
});

// POST /api/spec/:id/konvertera-till-impregnering – convert leverans spec → impregnering
router.post("/:id/konvertera-till-impregnering", async (req, res) => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
  if (isNaN(id)) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Ogiltigt id" });

  const [spec] = await db.select().from(specifikationerTable).where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, id));
  if (!spec) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Spec ej hittad" });
  if (spec.typ === "impregnering") return res.status(400).json({ error: "Redan en impregneringsorder" });
  if (spec.status === "bekraftad") return res.status(400).json({ error: "Bekräftade specar kan inte konverteras — återöppna först" });

  await db
    .update(specifikationerTable)
    .set({ typ: "impregnering", uppdateradAt: new Date() })
    .where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, id));

  const [updated] = await db.select().from(specifikationerTable).where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, id));
  res.json(updated);
});

// DELETE /api/spec/:id – delete spec
router.delete("/:id", async (req, res) => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
  if (isNaN(id)) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Ogiltigt id" });

  const [spec] = await db.select().from(specifikationerTable).where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, id));
  if (!spec) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Spec hittades inte" });
  if (spec.status === "bekraftad") return res.status(403).json({ error: "Bekräftade specifikationer kan inte raderas — återöppna den först" });

  await db.delete(specPaketTable).where(eq(specPaketTable.specId, id));
  await db.delete(specifikationerTable).where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, id));
  res.json({ ok: true });
});

// POST /api/spec/:id/bekrafta – confirm spec → mark packages delivered
router.post("/:id/bekrafta", async (req, res) => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
  if (isNaN(id)) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Ogiltigt id" });

  const [spec] = await db.select().from(specifikationerTable).where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, id));
  if (!spec) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Spec ej hittad" });
  if (!hasSenderSnapshot(spec)) {
    const [branding] = await db.select().from(brandingSettingsTable).where(eq(brandingSettingsTable.id, 1)).limit(1);
    const missing = branding ? missingDocumentProfileFields(brandingToDocumentProfile(branding)) : ["företagsprofil"];
    if (missing.length > 0) {
      return res.status(409).json({
        error: `Dokumentet kan inte bekräftas eftersom företagsprofilen är ofullständig. Fyll i ${missing.join(", ")} under Systeminställningar.`,
      });
    }
  }

  const today = new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];

  const paketRows = await db
    .select({ lagerPostId: specPaketTable.lagerPostId })
    .from(specPaketTable)
    .where(eq(specPaketTable.specId, id));

  const isHyvling = spec.typ === "hyvling";
  const behandlingTyp = behandlingTypFor(spec);
  const isTreatment = behandlingTyp !== null;

  if (isTreatment) {
    if (behandlingTyp === "IMPREGNERING" && !["NTR_A", "NTR_AB"].includes(spec.ntrKlass ?? "")) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: "Impregnering måste ha NTR-A eller NTR-AB vid bekräftelse" });
    }
    if (behandlingTyp === "IMPREGNERING" && spec.olikaNtrPerPaket) {
      const packageNtrRows = await db
        .select({ ntrKlass: specPaketTable.ntrKlass })
        .from(specPaketTable)
        .where(eq(specPaketTable.specId, id));
      if (packageNtrRows.some(({ ntrKlass }) => !["NTR_A", "NTR_AB"].includes(ntrKlass ?? ""))) {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: "Alla paket måste ha NTR-A eller NTR-AB vid bekräftelse" });
      }
    }
    // Treatment packages are temporarily unavailable, without a delivery date.
    for (const { lagerPostId } of paketRows) {
      await db
        .update(lagerPosterTable)
        .set({ avrappTyp: BEHANDLING_AVRAPP_TYP })
        .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.id, lagerPostId));
      await logHistorik(lagerPostId, "behandling_ut", { specId: id, behandlingTyp });
    }
    await db
      .update(specifikationerTable)
      .set({ status: "bekraftad", bekraftadAt: new Date(), uppdateradAt: new Date() })
      .where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, id));
    // Spara köpare automatiskt om det finns ett kundnamn
    await upsertKund(spec.kund);
    const [updated] = await db.select().from(specifikationerTable).where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, id));
    await captureSpecSenderSnapshot(spec);
    const [snapshotted] = await db.select().from(specifikationerTable).where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, id));
    return res.json(await mapSpecWithSender(snapshotted));
  }

  // Om en annan spec pekar på DENNA spec som kopplatSpecId → denna spec är råvara/intern transfer
  // och ska inte räknas i "levererat ut"-statistiken (undviker dubbelvolym)
  const reverseLinks = await db
    .select({ id: specifikationerTable.id })
    .from(specifikationerTable)
    .where(eq(specifikationerTable.kopplatSpecId, id));
  const isKallSpec = reverseLinks.length > 0;

  const avrappTyp = (isHyvling || isKallSpec) ? "hyvlat_till_lager" : "utlevererat";

  for (const { lagerPostId } of paketRows) {
    await db
      .update(lagerPosterTable)
      .set({ datumUt: today, avrappTyp })
      .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.id, lagerPostId));
    await logHistorik(lagerPostId, isHyvling ? "hyvlad" : "avrapportera", { specId: id, datumUt: today });
  }

  await db
    .update(specifikationerTable)
    .set({ status: "bekraftad", bekraftadAt: new Date(), uppdateradAt: new Date() })
    .where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, id));
  await captureSpecSenderSnapshot(spec);

  // Spara köpare automatiskt om det finns ett kundnamn
  await upsertKund(spec.kund);

  const [updated] = await db.select().from(specifikationerTable).where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, id));
  res.json(await mapSpecWithSender(updated));
});

// POST /api/spec/:id/ateroppen – reopen confirmed spec → restore packages to i lager
router.post("/:id/ateroppen", async (req, res) => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
  if (isNaN(id)) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Ogiltigt id" });

  const [spec] = await db.select().from(specifikationerTable).where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, id));
  if (!spec) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Spec ej hittad" });

  const paketRows = await db
    .select({ lagerPostId: specPaketTable.lagerPostId })
    .from(specPaketTable)
    .where(eq(specPaketTable.specId, id));

  const isTreatment = isBehandling(spec);
  for (const { lagerPostId } of paketRows) {
    await db
      .update(lagerPosterTable)
      .set(isTreatment ? { avrappTyp: null } : { datumUt: null, avrappTyp: null })
      .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.id, lagerPostId));
    await logHistorik(lagerPostId, "ateroppen", { specId: id });
  }

  await db
    .update(specifikationerTable)
    .set({ status: "utkast", bekraftadAt: null, uppdateradAt: new Date() })
    .where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, id));

  res.json({ ok: true, ateroppnade: paketRows.length });
});

// POST /api/spec/:id/returnera – paket returneras från impregnerare → tillbaka i_lager, spec stängs
router.post("/:id/returnera", async (req, res) => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
  if (isNaN(id)) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Ogiltigt id" });

  const [spec] = await db.select().from(specifikationerTable).where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, id));
  if (!spec) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Spec ej hittad" });
  if (!isBehandling(spec)) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Endast behandlingsspec kan returneras" });
  if (spec.status !== "bekraftad") return res.status(400).json({ error: "Spec måste vara bekräftad för retur" });

  const paketRows = await db
    .select({ lagerPostId: specPaketTable.lagerPostId })
    .from(specPaketTable)
    .where(eq(specPaketTable.specId, id));

  for (const { lagerPostId } of paketRows) {
    await db
      .update(lagerPosterTable)
      .set({ avrappTyp: null })
      .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.id, lagerPostId));
    await logHistorik(lagerPostId, "behandling_retur", { specId: id, behandlingTyp: behandlingTypFor(spec) });
  }

  await db
    .update(specifikationerTable)
    .set({ status: "returnerad", uppdateradAt: new Date() })
    .where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, id));

  res.json({ ok: true, returnerade: paketRows.length });
});

export default router;


================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/storage.ts ================

import { Readable } from "stream";
import { Router } from "express";
import { requireAdmin } from "../app";
import { ObjectNotFoundError, ObjectStorageService } from "../lib/objectStorage";

const router = Router();
const objectStorageService = new ObjectStorageService();

router.post("/storage/uploads/request-url", requireAdmin, async (req: any, res) => {
  const { name, size, contentType } = req.body ?? {};
  if (
    typeof name !== "string" ||
    name.trim().length === 0 ||
    typeof size !== "number" ||
    size < 1 ||
    size > 5 * 1024 * 1024 ||
    typeof contentType !== "string" ||
    !["image/png", "image/jpeg", "image/webp", "image/svg+xml"].includes(contentType)
  ) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Välj en PNG-, JPG-, WebP- eller SVG-logotyp på högst 5 MB." });
    return;
  }

  try {
    const uploadURL = await objectStorageService.getObjectEntityUploadURL();
    res.json({
      uploadURL,
      objectPath: objectStorageService.normalizeObjectEntityPath(uploadURL),
      metadata: { name, size, contentType },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    req.log.error({ err: error }, "Kunde inte skapa uppladdningsadress");
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Kunde inte förbereda logotypuppladdningen." });
  }
});

router.get("/storage/objects/*path", async (req: any, res) => {
  try {
    const raw = req.params.path;
    const path = `/objects/${Array.isArray(raw) ? raw.join("/") : raw}`;
    const response = await objectStorageService.downloadObject(
      await objectStorageService.getObjectEntityFile(path),
    );
    res.status(response.status);
    response.headers.forEach((value, key) => res.setHeader(key, value));
    if (response.body) {
      Readable.fromWeb(response.body as ReadableStream<Uint8Array>).pipe(res);
    } else {
      res.end();
    }
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ObjectNotFoundError) {
      res.status(404).json({ error: "Objektet finns inte." });
      return;
    }
    req.log.error({ err: error }, "Kunde inte läsa objekt");
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Kunde inte läsa objektet." });
  }
});

export default router;

================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/sync.ts ================

import { Router, Request, Response } from "express";
import { db, lagerPosterTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";

const router = Router();

const ETIKETT_API = "https://emmabodahyvleri-etikett.on-forge.com/api/packages";

interface EtikettLength {
  length: number;
  quantity: number;
}

interface EtikettPackage {
  id: number;
  package_number: string;
  dimension: string;
  profile: string;
  wood_type: string;
  volume: number;
  label_note: string;
  notes: string;
  created_at: string;
  is_template: number;
  lengths: EtikettLength[];
}

function parseDimension(dim: string): { tjocklek: number | null; bredd: number | null } {
  const parts = dim.split("x").map((p) => parseInt(p.trim(), 10));
  if (parts.length === 2 && !isNaN(parts[0]) && !isNaN(parts[1])) {
    return { tjocklek: parts[0], bredd: parts[1] };
  }
  return { tjocklek: null, bredd: null };
}

router.post("/sync/etikett", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const response = await fetch(ETIKETT_API);
    if (!response.ok) {
      res.status(502).json({ error: "Kunde inte nå etikettrogrammet" });
      return;
    }

    const packages: EtikettPackage[] = await response.json();

    let nySkapade = 0;
    let uppdaterade = 0;
    let hoppadeOver = 0;

    for (const pkg of packages) {
      if (pkg.is_template === 1) {
        hoppadeOver++;
        continue;
      }

      const { tjocklek, bredd } = parseDimension(pkg.dimension);

      const totalAntal = pkg.lengths.reduce((sum, l) => sum + l.quantity, 0);
      const firstLength = pkg.lengths[0];
      const langdDm = firstLength ? Math.round(firstLength.length / 100) : null;

      let lpm: number | null = null;
      if (langdDm && totalAntal) {
        lpm = parseFloat(((langdDm / 10) * totalAntal).toFixed(2));
      }

      const datumIn = pkg.created_at.split(" ")[0];
      const anteckning = [pkg.label_note, pkg.notes].filter(Boolean).join(" | ") || null;

      const values = {
        pktNr: pkg.package_number,
        varuslag: pkg.wood_type || null,
        profil: pkg.profile || null,
        nomTjocklek: tjocklek?.toString() ?? null,
        nomBredd: bredd?.toString() ?? null,
        langdDm: langdDm?.toString() ?? null,
        antal: totalAntal > 0 ? totalAntal : null,
        m3: pkg.volume > 0 ? pkg.volume.toString() : null,
        lpm: lpm?.toString() ?? null,
        datumIn,
        anteckning,
        produktion: "Paketlapp",
      };

      const existing = await db
        .select()
        .from(lagerPosterTable)
        .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.pktNr, pkg.package_number))
        .limit(1);

      if (existing[0]) {
        await db
          .update(lagerPosterTable)
          .set({ ...values, uppdateradAt: new Date() })
          .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.id, existing[0].id));
        uppdaterade++;
      } else {
        await db.insert(lagerPosterTable).values(values);
        nySkapade++;
      }
    }

    res.json({ nySkapade, uppdaterade, hoppadeOver, total: packages.length });
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error("Sync error:", err);
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Synkfel", details: err.message });
  }
});

export default router;


================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/vilmabas.ts ================

import { Router } from "express";
import { db, vilmabasArtiklar } from "@workspace/db";
import { ilike, or, sql } from "drizzle-orm";

const router = Router();

router.get("/", async (req, res) => {
  const q = String(req.query["q"] ?? "").trim();

  if (!q) {
    const rows = await db.select().from(vilmabasArtiklar).limit(20);
    res.json(rows);
    return;
  }

  const term = `%${q}%`;
  const rows = await db
    .select()
    .from(vilmabasArtiklar)
    .where(
      or(
        ilike(vilmabasArtiklar.vilmabasId, term),
        ilike(vilmabasArtiklar.namn, term),
        sql`CONCAT(${vilmabasArtiklar.tjocklek}, 'x', ${vilmabasArtiklar.totalBradd}) ILIKE ${term}`,
        ilike(vilmabasArtiklar.skarProfil, term),
        ilike(vilmabasArtiklar.traslag, term),
        ilike(vilmabasArtiklar.varugrupp, term),
      )
    )
    .limit(30);

  res.json(rows);
});

export default router;


================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/seed.ts ================

import { db, lagerPosterTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { readFileSync } from "fs";
import { join } from "path";

function parseVal(val: string): string | null {
  if (val === "" || val === "\\N") return null;
  return val;
}

function parseNum(val: string): number | null {
  if (val === "" || val === "\\N") return null;
  const n = parseFloat(val);
  return isNaN(n) ? null : n;
}

export async function seedIfEmpty() {
  const result = await db.execute(sql`SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM lager_poster`);
  const count = Number((result.rows[0] as { count: string }).count);

  if (count >= 2000) {
    return;
  }

  if (count > 0) {
    console.log(`[seed] Only ${count} rows found, truncating and re-seeding...`);
    await db.execute(sql`TRUNCATE TABLE lager_poster RESTART IDENTITY CASCADE`);
  }

  console.log("[seed] Production DB is empty, seeding from CSV...");

  const seedPath = join(__dirname, "lager_seed.csv");
  const lines = readFileSync(seedPath, "utf-8").split("\n").filter(Boolean);

  const batchSize = 50;
  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i += batchSize) {
    const batch = lines.slice(i, i + batchSize).map((line) => {
      const cols = line.split("|");
      return {
        datumIn: parseVal(cols[0]),
        datumUt: parseVal(cols[1]),
        mottaget: parseVal(cols[2]),
        plats: parseVal(cols[3]),
        anmarkNing: parseVal(cols[4]),
        pktNr: parseVal(cols[5]),
        produktion: parseVal(cols[6]),
        profil: parseVal(cols[7]),
        varuslag: parseVal(cols[8]),
        traslag: parseVal(cols[9]),
        kvalitet: parseVal(cols[10]),
        avrapp: parseVal(cols[11]),
        nomTjocklek: parseNum(cols[12]),
        nomBredd: parseNum(cols[13]),
        actTjocklek: parseNum(cols[14]),
        actBredd: parseNum(cols[15]),
        antal: parseNum(cols[16]),
        langdDm: parseNum(cols[17]),
        m3: parseNum(cols[18]),
        m3Pris: parseNum(cols[19]),
        m3Sum: parseNum(cols[20]),
        avrappM3: parseNum(cols[21]),
        lpm: parseNum(cols[22]),
        actM3: parseNum(cols[23]),
        anteckning: parseVal(cols[24]),
      };
    });

    await db.insert(lagerPosterTable).values(batch);
    console.log(`[seed] Inserted rows ${i + 1}–${Math.min(i + batchSize, lines.length)}`);
  }

  console.log("[seed] Done — 851 rows inserted.");
}

================ FILE: artifacts/lager/dev-proxy.mjs ================

#!/usr/bin/env node
import http from "http";
import net from "net";

const VITE_PORT = 5173;
const API_PORT = 3001;
const PROXY_PORT = parseInt(process.env.DEV_PROXY_PORT || "8080", 10);

function isApiPath(url) {
  return (
    url.startsWith("/api") ||
    url === "/config.js" ||
    url.startsWith("/config.js?")
  );
}

const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
  const targetPort = isApiPath(req.url || "") ? API_PORT : VITE_PORT;
  const options = {
    hostname: "127.0.0.1",
    port: targetPort,
    path: req.url,
    method: req.method,
    headers: { ...req.headers, host: `localhost:${targetPort}` },
  };

  const proxyReq = http.request(options, (proxyRes) => {
    res.writeHead(proxyRes.statusCode ?? 502, proxyRes.headers);
    proxyRes.pipe(res, { end: true });
  });

  proxyReq.on("error", (err) => {
    if (!res.headersSent) {
      res.writeHead(502, { "Content-Type": "text/plain" });
    }
    res.end(`Bad Gateway: ${err.message}`);
  });

  req.pipe(proxyReq, { end: true });
});

server.on("upgrade", (req, clientSocket, head) => {
  const targetPort = isApiPath(req.url || "") ? API_PORT : VITE_PORT;
  const serverSocket = net.createConnection({
    port: targetPort,
    host: "127.0.0.1",
  });

  serverSocket.on("connect", () => {
    const headerLines = [
      `${req.method} ${req.url} HTTP/1.1`,
      ...Object.entries(req.headers).map(
        ([k, v]) => `${k}: ${Array.isArray(v) ? v.join(", ") : v}`
      ),
      "",
      "",
    ].join("\r\n");
    serverSocket.write(headerLines);
    if (head?.length) serverSocket.write(head);
    serverSocket.pipe(clientSocket);
    clientSocket.pipe(serverSocket);
  });

  serverSocket.on("error", () => clientSocket.destroy());
  clientSocket.on("error", () => serverSocket.destroy());
});

server.listen(PROXY_PORT, "0.0.0.0", () => {
  console.log(
    `[dev-proxy] ✓ http://localhost:${PROXY_PORT}  →  Vite:${VITE_PORT} | API:${API_PORT}`
  );
});

import { lazy, Suspense, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Switch, Route, Router as WouterRouter, Redirect, useLocation } from "wouter";
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider, QueryCache, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { ClerkProvider, ClerkLoading, SignIn, SignUp, Show, useClerk, useAuth } from "@clerk/react";
import { Toaster } from "@/components/ui/toaster";
import { TooltipProvider } from "@/components/ui/tooltip";
import { setAuthTokenGetter, ApiError } from "@workspace/api-client-react";

import { Layout } from "@/components/layout/Layout";
import { BrandingProvider, useBranding } from "@/components/branding/BrandingContext";

const NotFound               = lazy(() => import("@/pages/not-found"));
const IngenAtkomstPage       = lazy(() => import("@/pages/IngenAtkomstPage"));
const Dashboard              = lazy(() => import("@/pages/dashboard/Dashboard"));

const InventoryForm          = lazy(() => import("@/pages/inventory/InventoryForm"));
const InventoryDetail        = lazy(() => import("@/pages/inventory/InventoryDetail"));

const OmgangarPage           = lazy(() => import("@/pages/inventering/OmgangarPage"));

const ForsaljningsstatistikPage = lazy(() => import("@/pages/forsaljningsstatistik/ForsaljningsstatistikPage"));

const clerkPubKey: string =
  (window as any).__CLERK_PUB_KEY__ ||
  import.meta.env.VITE_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY ||
  "";
const clerkProxyUrl = import.meta.env.VITE_CLERK_PROXY_URL;
const basePath = import.meta.env.BASE_URL.replace(/\/$/, "");
const defaultLogoPath = `${basePath}/pwa-512.png`;

if (!clerkPubKey) {
  throw new Error("Clerk-nyckel saknas. Sätt CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY i .env-filen.");
}

function stripBase(path: string): string {
  return basePath && path.startsWith(basePath)
    ? path.slice(basePath.length) || "/"
    : path;
}

const queryClient = new QueryClient({
  queryCache: new QueryCache({
    onError: (error) => {
      const base = import.meta.env.BASE_URL.replace(/\/$/, "");
      if (error instanceof ApiError && error.status === 401) {
        window.location.href = `${base}/sign-in`;
      } else if (error instanceof ApiError && error.status === 403) {
        window.location.href = `${base}/ingen-atkomst`;
      }
    },
  }),
  defaultOptions: {
    queries: {
      retry: (failureCount, error) => {
        if (error instanceof ApiError && (error.status === 401 || error.status === 403)) return false;
        return failureCount < 1;
      },
      refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
      staleTime: 30_000,
    },
  },
});

function ClerkQueryClientCacheInvalidator() {
  const { addListener } = useClerk();
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const prevUserIdRef = useRef<string | null | undefined>(undefined);

  useEffect(() => {
    const unsubscribe = addListener(({ user }) => {
      const userId = user?.id ?? null;
      if (prevUserIdRef.current !== undefined && prevUserIdRef.current !== userId) {
        qc.clear();
      }
      prevUserIdRef.current = userId;
    });
    return unsubscribe;
  }, [addListener, qc]);

  return null;
}

function ClerkAuthSetup() {
  const { getToken } = useAuth();

  useEffect(() => {
    setAuthTokenGetter(() => getToken());
    return () => setAuthTokenGetter(null);
  }, [getToken]);

  return null;
}

function PageSpinner() {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-center min-h-[40vh]">
      <div className="h-8 w-8 rounded-full border-4 border-[#2d5a27]/20 border-t-[#2d5a27] animate-spin" />
    </div>
  );
}

function BrandLogo({ className }: { className: string }) {
  const branding = useBranding();
  return <img src={branding.logoUrl ?? defaultLogoPath} alt={branding.companyName} className={className} />;
}

function AuthPageWrapper({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const branding = useBranding();

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4" style={{ background: "linear-gradient(160deg, #f0f4ef 0%, #ffffff 60%, #f7f7f5 100%)" }}>
      <div className="mb-8 flex flex-col items-center gap-3">
        <BrandLogo className="w-40 h-40 object-contain drop-shadow-sm" />
        <div className="h-px w-32 bg-[#2d5a27]/20 mt-1" />
        <div className="text-sm text-[#2d5a27]/70 font-medium tracking-widest uppercase">{branding.companyName}</div>
      </div>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

function SignInPage() {
  return (
    <AuthPageWrapper>
      <SignIn
        routing="path"
        path={`${basePath}/sign-in`}
        fallbackRedirectUrl={`${basePath}/`}
      />
    </AuthPageWrapper>
  );
}

function SignUpPage() {
  return (
    <AuthPageWrapper>
      <SignUp
        routing="path"
        path={`${basePath}/sign-up`}
        fallbackRedirectUrl={`${basePath}/`}
        signInUrl={`${basePath}/sign-in`}
      />
    </AuthPageWrapper>
  );
}


function LandingPage() {
  const branding = useBranding();

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4" style={{ background: "linear-gradient(160deg, #f0f4ef 0%, #ffffff 60%, #f7f7f5 100%)" }}>
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-6 text-center max-w-sm">
        <BrandLogo className="w-52 h-52 object-contain drop-shadow-md" />
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <div className="h-px w-40 mx-auto bg-[#2d5a27]/20" />
          <div className="text-xs text-[#2d5a27]/60 font-medium tracking-widest uppercase pt-2">{branding.companyName}</div>
        </div>
        <a
          href={`${basePath}/sign-in`}
          className="w-full max-w-[200px] inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-[#2d5a27] px-8 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-[#224420] transition-colors shadow-sm"
        >
          Logga in
        </a>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function HomeRoute() {
  return (
    <>
      <Show when="signed-in">
        <Layout>
          <Suspense fallback={<PageSpinner />}>
            <Dashboard />
          </Suspense>
        </Layout>
      </Show>
      <Show when="signed-out">
        <LandingPage />
      </Show>
    </>
  );
}

function ProtectedRoute({ component: Component }: { component: React.ComponentType }) {
  return (
    <>
      <Show when="signed-in">
        <Layout>
          <Suspense fallback={<PageSpinner />}>
            <Component />
          </Suspense>
        </Layout>
      </Show>
      <Show when="signed-out">
        <Redirect to="/" />
      </Show>
    </>
  );
}

function Router() {
  return (
    <Switch>
      <Route path="/" component={HomeRoute} />
      <Route path="/sign-in/*?" component={SignInPage} />
      <Route path="/sign-up/*?" component={SignUpPage} />
      <Route path="/ingen-atkomst" component={() => <Suspense fallback={<PageSpinner />}><IngenAtkomstPage /></Suspense>} />
      <Route path="/lager" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={InventoryList} />} />
      <Route path="/lager/ny" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={InventoryForm} />} />
      <Route path="/lager/:id/redigera" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={InventoryForm} />} />
      <Route path="/lager/:id" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={InventoryDetail} />} />
      <Route path="/spec" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={() => <SpecList typ="leverans" />} />} />
      <Route path="/spec/ny" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={() => <SpecPage typ="leverans" />} />} />
      <Route path="/spec/:id" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={() => <SpecPage typ="leverans" />} />} />
      <Route path="/hyvling" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={() => <SpecList typ="hyvling" />} />} />
      <Route path="/hyvling/ny" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={() => <SpecPage typ="hyvling" />} />} />
      <Route path="/hyvling/:id" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={() => <SpecPage typ="hyvling" />} />} />
      <Route path="/lagerplatser" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={LagerplatsPage} />} />
      <Route path="/lagerplatser/omrade/:slug" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={OmradeDetaljPage} />} />
      <Route path="/inventering" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={InventeringOversikt} />} />
      <Route path="/inventering/omgangar" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={OmgangarPage} />} />
      <Route path="/inventering/omgangar/:id/rapport" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={InventeringsomgangRapportPage} />} />
      <Route path="/inventering/omgangar/:id" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={OmgangDetaljPage} />} />

      <Route path="/inventering/:id/rapport" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={InventeringRapportPage} />} />
      <Route path="/inventering/:id" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={InventeringDetaljPage} />} />
      <Route path="/larm" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={LarmPage} />} />
      <Route path="/historik" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={HistorikPage} />} />
      <Route path="/installningar" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={SysteminstallningarPage} />} />
      <Route path="/rapport" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={RapportPage} />} />
      <Route path="/forsaljningsstatistik" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={ForsaljningsstatistikPage} />} />
      <Route path="/prislistor" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={PrislistorPage} />} />
      <Route path="/paketlapp-preview/:id" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={PaketlappPreview} />} />
      <Route path="/lego" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={LegoList} />} />

      <Route path="/skanna-ut" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={SkannaUtPage} />} />
      <Route path="/papperskorg" component={() => <ProtectedRoute component={PapperskorgPage} />} />
      <Route component={() => <Suspense fallback={<PageSpinner />}><NotFound /></Suspense>} />
    </Switch>
  );
}

function ClerkProviderWithRoutes() {
  const [, setLocation] = useLocation();

  return (
    <ClerkProvider
      publishableKey={clerkPubKey}
      proxyUrl={clerkProxyUrl}
      signInUrl={`${basePath}/sign-in`}
      signUpUrl={`${basePath}/sign-up`}
      routerPush={(to) => setLocation(stripBase(to))}
      routerReplace={(to) => setLocation(stripBase(to), { replace: true })}
    >
      <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
        <ClerkAuthSetup />
        <ClerkQueryClientCacheInvalidator />
        <TooltipProvider>
          <BrandingProvider>
            <ClerkLoading>
              <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-4" style={{ background: "linear-gradient(160deg, #f0f4ef 0%, #ffffff 60%, #f7f7f5 100%)" }}>
                <BrandLogo className="w-24 h-24 object-contain opacity-80" />
                <div className="h-8 w-8 rounded-full border-4 border-[#2d5a27]/20 border-t-[#2d5a27] animate-spin" />
              </div>
            </ClerkLoading>
            <Router />
          </BrandingProvider>
          <Toaster />
        </TooltipProvider>
      </QueryClientProvider>
    </ClerkProvider>
  );
}

function App() {
  return (
    <WouterRouter base={basePath}>
      <ClerkProviderWithRoutes />
    </WouterRouter>
  );
}

export default App;


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/components/DashboardAlerts.tsx ================

import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { customFetch } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Package, Layers, Wrench, BellRing, Droplets } from "lucide-react";
import { useLocation } from "wouter";

type DashboardAlertsData = {
  antalPaketILager: number;
  m3ILager: number;
  aktivaHyvlingsordrar: number;
  triggeradeLarm: number;
  forsendadeImpregnering: number;
  impDagarGrans: number;
};

type Variant = "green" | "yellow" | "red";

const VARIANT_STYLES: Record<Variant, {
  border: string;
  bg: string;
  iconCls: string;
  valueCls: string;
  badgeText: string | null;
  badgeCls: string;
}> = {
  green:  { border: "border-green-200",  bg: "bg-green-50/40",  iconCls: "text-green-600",  valueCls: "text-green-700",  badgeText: null,      badgeCls: "" },
  yellow: { border: "border-amber-200",  bg: "bg-amber-50/40",  iconCls: "text-amber-600",  valueCls: "text-amber-700",  badgeText: "Aktiv",   badgeCls: "bg-amber-100 text-amber-800" },
  red:    { border: "border-red-200",    bg: "bg-red-50/40",    iconCls: "text-red-600",    valueCls: "text-red-700",    badgeText: "Varning", badgeCls: "bg-red-100 text-red-800" },
};

function AlertCard({
  title,
  value,
  sub,
  variant,
  icon: Icon,
  onClick,
}: {
  title: string;
  value: string;
  sub: string;
  variant: Variant;
  icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
  onClick?: () => void;
}) {
  const s = VARIANT_STYLES[variant];
  return (
    <Card
      className={`${s.border} ${s.bg} ${onClick ? "cursor-pointer hover:shadow-md transition-shadow" : ""}`}
      onClick={onClick}
    >
      <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between space-y-0 pb-2">
        <CardTitle className="text-sm font-medium">{title}</CardTitle>
        <Icon className={`h-4 w-4 ${s.iconCls}`} />
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent>
        <div className={`text-2xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2 ${s.valueCls}`}>
          {value}
          {s.badgeText && (
            <span className={`text-xs font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${s.badgeCls}`}>
              {s.badgeText}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">
          {sub}
          {onClick ? " — klicka för detaljer" : ""}
        </p>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

export default function DashboardAlerts() {
  const [, navigate] = useLocation();

  const { data, isLoading } = useQuery<DashboardAlertsData>({
    queryKey: ["/api/lager/dashboard-alerts"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<DashboardAlertsData>("/api/lager/dashboard-alerts"),
    refetchInterval: 60_000,
    staleTime: 30_000,
    refetchOnWindowFocus: true,
  });

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="grid gap-3 grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-5">
        {[...Array(5)].map((_, i) => (
          <Skeleton key={i} className="h-28 rounded-xl" />
        ))}
      </div>
    );
  }

  const d: DashboardAlertsData = data ?? {
    antalPaketILager: 0,
    m3ILager: 0,
    aktivaHyvlingsordrar: 0,
    triggeradeLarm: 0,
    forsendadeImpregnering: 0,
    impDagarGrans: 14,
  };

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-3 grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-5">
      <AlertCard
        title="Paket i lager"
        value={`${d.antalPaketILager} st`}
        sub="Aktiva paket i lager"
        variant="green"
        icon={Package}
      />
      <AlertCard
        title="m³ i lager"
        value={`${d.m3ILager.toFixed(2)} m³`}
        sub="Total aktiv volym"
        variant="green"
        icon={Layers}
      />
      <AlertCard
        title="Hyvlingsordrar"
        value={`${d.aktivaHyvlingsordrar} st`}
        sub={d.aktivaHyvlingsordrar === 0 ? "Inga aktiva ordrar" : "Ej klara ordrar"}
        variant={d.aktivaHyvlingsordrar > 0 ? "yellow" : "green"}
        icon={Wrench}
        onClick={d.aktivaHyvlingsordrar > 0 ? () => navigate("/hyvling") : undefined}
      />
      <AlertCard
        title="Lagervarningar"
        value={`${d.triggeradeLarm} st`}
        sub={d.triggeradeLarm === 0 ? "Inga aktiva larm" : "Under larmgräns"}
        variant={d.triggeradeLarm > 0 ? "red" : "green"}
        icon={BellRing}
        onClick={() => navigate("/larm")}
      />
      <AlertCard
        title="Försenad behandling"
        value={`${d.forsendadeImpregnering} st`}
        sub={
          d.forsendadeImpregnering === 0
            ? `Inga försenade (>${d.impDagarGrans} dagar)`
            : `Hos behandling >${d.impDagarGrans} dagar`
        }
        variant={d.forsendadeImpregnering > 0 ? "red" : "green"}
        icon={Droplets}
        onClick={d.forsendadeImpregnering > 0 ? () => navigate("/behandlingar") : undefined}
      />
    </div>
  );
}


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/components/LagerkartaSVG.tsx ================

import { useState } from "react";
import { useLocation } from "wouter";
import { useBranding } from "@/components/branding/BrandingContext";

interface PlatsStat {
  namn: string;
  m3: number;
  count: number;
  pkt: number;
}

interface LagerkartaSVGProps {
  platserStats: PlatsStat[];
  /**
   * Statistik från det strukturerade område-API:t, nyckel = områdesslug.
   * Zoner med `detailSlug` läser sin statistik härifrån (auktoritativ källa
   * för områden med fack) istället för fritext-gruppering på `plats`, så
   * att hover-siffrorna alltid matchar detaljvyn/Ändra plats-dialogen även
   * när gammal och ny plats-text finns blandat inom samma område (t.ex.
   * Kontorsmagasin under övergången från "Hyvleriet").
   */
  omradeStatsBySlug?: Record<string, PlatsStat>;
  omgangId?: number;
  inventeringsprogressBySlug?: Record<string, { klara: number; totalt: number }>;
}

interface ZoneDef {
  id: string;
  platsNamn: string;
  lines: string[];
  path: string;
  labelX: number;
  labelY: number;
  color: string;
  hoverColor: string;
  textColor: string;
  /** Om satt: klick öppnar detaljkartan för området (omraden/:slug) istället för fritext-filtrering. */
  detailSlug?: string;
  /**
   * Om satt: denna SVG-form delar hover/highlight med andra zoner som har
   * samma groupId, eftersom de representerar SAMMA lagerområde uppdelat i
   * flera visuella ytor (t.ex. "Runt Röda Magasin" som ligger både väster
   * och öster om själva Röda Magasin-byggnaden). Standard = zonens eget id.
   */
  groupId?: string;
}

function zoneGroupKey(zone: ZoneDef): string {
  return zone.groupId ?? zone.id;
}

interface StaticBuildingDef {
  id: string;
  path: string;
  label?: string;
  labelX?: number;
  labelY?: number;
}
