// Non-storage buildings (visual only, not clickable lagerplatser)
const STATIC_BUILDINGS: StaticBuildingDef[] = [
  // Sågverk main complex – top-left L-shape
  { id: "saagverk-block", path: "M 0,0 L 125,0 L 125,172 L 0,172 Z" },
  // Sågverk long hall running E-W
  { id: "saagverk-hall", path: "M 98,5 L 390,5 L 390,118 L 98,118 Z" },
  // Lower-right building cluster (below Pannan/Järnväg MC)
  { id: "lower-right", path: "M 468,404 L 644,404 L 644,508 L 468,508 Z" },
  // Bottom long storage building (below lastplan, along bottom edge)
  { id: "bottom-storage", path: "M 240,758 L 455,758 L 455,848 L 240,848 Z" },
];

// Road/yard polygons – slightly different shade from background
const ROADS = [
  // Central N-S yard corridor
  "M 148,172 L 240,172 L 240,900 L 148,900 Z",
  // E-W yard corridor (mid-level)
  "M 0,505 L 780,505 L 780,530 L 0,530 Z",
  // Upper yard between sågverk and Graniten
  "M 390,5 L 516,5 L 516,108 L 390,108 L 390,5 Z",
];

// Railway track path (right edge, diagonal)
const RAILWAY_PATH = "M 720,52 L 780,52 L 780,900 L 720,900 Z";
const RAILWAY_TIES: [number, number][] = Array.from({ length: 28 }, (_, i) => [720, 60 + i * 30]);

// Vegetation patches (trees/shrubs around perimeter)
const VEGETATION_PATCHES = [
  "M 0,172 L 148,172 L 148,345 L 0,345 Z",
  "M 0,345 L 52,345 L 52,900 L 0,900 Z",
  "M 308,670 L 455,670 L 455,758 L 308,758 Z",
  "M 52,758 L 240,758 L 240,848 L 52,848 Z",
  "M 52,848 L 455,848 L 455,900 L 52,900 Z",
  "M 455,615 L 720,615 L 720,900 L 455,900 Z",
  "M 552,108 L 516,108 L 516,52 L 552,52 Z",
];

export function LagerkartaSVG({ platserStats, omradeStatsBySlug, omgangId, inventeringsprogressBySlug }: LagerkartaSVGProps) {
  const branding = useBranding();
  const [, setLocation] = useLocation();
  const [hoveredZone, setHoveredZone] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [tooltip, setTooltip] = useState<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);

  const statsByNamn = Object.fromEntries(platserStats.map((s) => [s.namn, s]));

  const handleZoneClick = (zone: ZoneDef) => {
    if (zone.detailSlug) {
      setLocation(`/lagerplatser/omrade/${zone.detailSlug}${omgangId ? `?omgangId=${omgangId}` : ""}`);
      return;
    }
    setLocation(`/lager?plats=${encodeURIComponent(zone.platsNamn)}`);
  };

  function statFor(zone: ZoneDef): PlatsStat | undefined {
    if (zone.detailSlug && omradeStatsBySlug) return omradeStatsBySlug[zone.detailSlug];
    return statsByNamn[zone.platsNamn];
  }

  const hoveredZoneDef = ZONES.find((z) => zoneGroupKey(z) === hoveredZone);
  const hoveredStat = hoveredZoneDef ? statFor(hoveredZoneDef) : null;

  return (
    <div className="relative w-full rounded-xl overflow-hidden border bg-[#2A2A2A] shadow-lg">
      <svg
        viewBox="0 0 780 900"
        className="w-full h-auto block"
        style={{ maxHeight: "80vh" }}
        xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
      >
        <defs>
          <filter id="shadow" x="-10%" y="-10%" width="120%" height="120%">
            <feDropShadow dx="0" dy="2" stdDeviation="3" floodColor="#00000040" />
          </filter>
          <filter id="glow">
            <feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="3" result="blur" />
            <feMerge>
              <feMergeNode in="blur" />
              <feMergeNode in="SourceGraphic" />
            </feMerge>
          </filter>
          <pattern id="hatch" patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse" width="10" height="10" patternTransform="rotate(45)">
            <line x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="10" stroke="#A08040" strokeWidth="2" opacity="0.4" />
          </pattern>
        </defs>

        {/* ── Background ground / yard ── */}
        <rect x="0" y="0" width="780" height="900" fill="#D4CDBF" />

        {/* ── Vegetation patches ── */}
        {VEGETATION_PATCHES.map((d, i) => (
          <path key={i} d={d} fill="#8EA870" opacity="0.7" />
        ))}

        {/* ── Roads / yard corridors ── */}
        {ROADS.map((d, i) => (
          <path key={i} d={d} fill="#C4BDB0" />
        ))}

        {/* ── Railway zone ── */}
        <path d={RAILWAY_PATH} fill="#A0A0A8" opacity="0.6" />
        {/* Railway track lines */}
        <line x1="735" y1="52" x2="735" y2="900" stroke="#666" strokeWidth="3" strokeDasharray="none" />
        <line x1="755" y1="52" x2="755" y2="900" stroke="#666" strokeWidth="3" />
        {/* Cross-ties */}
        {RAILWAY_TIES.map(([x, y], i) => (
          <line key={i} x1={x} y1={y} x2={x + 40} y2={y} stroke="#555" strokeWidth="2.5" />
        ))}
        <text x="750" y="560" fill="#444" fontSize="10" fontFamily="system-ui" fontWeight="600"
          transform="rotate(-90, 750, 560)" textAnchor="middle" letterSpacing="2">
          JÄRNVÄG
        </text>

        {/* ── Static buildings (production / utility) ── */}
        {STATIC_BUILDINGS.map((b) => (
          <g key={b.id}>
            <path d={b.path} fill="#7E8E9C" stroke="#5A6A76" strokeWidth="1.5" />
            {b.label && b.labelX && b.labelY && (
              <text
                x={b.labelX} y={b.labelY}
                fill="#E0E8F0" fontSize="11" fontFamily="system-ui"
                fontWeight="600" textAnchor="middle" dominantBaseline="middle"
                letterSpacing="0.5"
              >
                {b.label}
              </text>
            )}
          </g>
        ))}

        {/* Sågverk label (spans both blocks) */}
        <text x="200" y="62" fill="#C8D4DC" fontSize="13" fontFamily="system-ui"
          fontWeight="700" textAnchor="middle" dominantBaseline="middle" letterSpacing="1">
          SÅGVERK
        </text>

        {/* ── Clickable storage zones ── */}
        {ZONES.map((zone) => {
          const isHovered = hoveredZone === zoneGroupKey(zone);
          const fill = isHovered ? zone.hoverColor : zone.color;
          const stat = statFor(zone);
          const inventeringsprogress = zone.detailSlug ? inventeringsprogressBySlug?.[zone.detailSlug] : undefined;
          return (
            <g
              key={zone.id}
              onClick={() => handleZoneClick(zone)}
              onMouseEnter={(e) => {
                setHoveredZone(zoneGroupKey(zone));
                const rect = (e.currentTarget.closest("svg") as SVGSVGElement)?.getBoundingClientRect();
                if (rect) {
                  setTooltip({ x: e.clientX - rect.left, y: e.clientY - rect.top });
                }
              }}
              onMouseLeave={() => { setHoveredZone(null); setTooltip(null); }}
              onMouseMove={(e) => {
                const rect = (e.currentTarget.closest("svg") as SVGSVGElement)?.getBoundingClientRect();
                if (rect) setTooltip({ x: e.clientX - rect.left, y: e.clientY - rect.top });
              }}
              style={{ cursor: "pointer" }}
            >
              <path
                d={zone.path}
                fill={fill}
                stroke={isHovered ? "#FFFFFF" : "#FFFFFF50"}
                strokeWidth={isHovered ? 2.5 : 1.5}
                filter={isHovered ? "url(#shadow)" : undefined}
                style={{ transition: "fill 0.15s, stroke 0.15s" }}
              />
              {/* Hatching overlay for zones with no data */}
              {!stat && (
                <path d={zone.path} fill="url(#hatch)" opacity="0.3" />
              )}
              {/* Zone label */}
              {zone.lines.map((line, i) => (
                <text
                  key={i}
                  x={zone.labelX}
                  y={zone.labelY + (i - (zone.lines.length - 1) / 2) * 16}
                  fill={zone.textColor}
                  fontSize="13"
                  fontFamily="system-ui, sans-serif"
                  fontWeight="700"
                  textAnchor="middle"
                  dominantBaseline="middle"
                  style={{ pointerEvents: "none", userSelect: "none" }}
                  filter="url(#shadow)"
                >
                  {line}
                </text>
              ))}
              {/* Kompakt progress i inventeringsläge, annars befintlig m³-etikett. */}
              {inventeringsprogress ? (
                <text
                  x={zone.labelX}
                  y={zone.labelY + zone.lines.length * 9 + 6}
                  fill={zone.textColor}
                  fontSize="10"
                  fontFamily="system-ui, sans-serif"
                  fontWeight="700"
                  textAnchor="middle"
                  dominantBaseline="middle"
                  style={{ pointerEvents: "none", userSelect: "none" }}
                >
                  {inventeringsprogress.klara} / {inventeringsprogress.totalt} klara
                </text>
              ) : stat && (
                <text
                  x={zone.labelX}
                  y={zone.labelY + zone.lines.length * 9 + 6}
                  fill={zone.textColor}
                  fontSize="10"
                  fontFamily="system-ui, sans-serif"
                  fontWeight="500"
                  textAnchor="middle"
                  dominantBaseline="middle"
                  opacity="0.85"
                  style={{ pointerEvents: "none", userSelect: "none" }}
                >
                  {stat.m3.toFixed(0)} m³
                </text>
              )}
            </g>
          );
        })}

        {/* ── Compass rose (north arrow) ── */}
        <g transform="translate(30, 840)">
          <circle cx="0" cy="0" r="22" fill="#2A2A2A90" stroke="#FFFFFF40" strokeWidth="1" />
          {/* N arrow */}
          <polygon points="0,-16 -5,8 0,4 5,8" fill="#FFFFFF" />
          <polygon points="0,16 -5,-8 0,-4 5,-8" fill="#FFFFFF50" />
          <text x="0" y="-1" fill="white" fontSize="9" fontFamily="system-ui" fontWeight="800"
            textAnchor="middle" dominantBaseline="middle">N</text>
        </g>

        {/* ── Legend ── */}
        <g transform="translate(490, 760)">
          <rect x="0" y="0" width="220" height="118" rx="6" fill="#1A1A1A90" stroke="#FFFFFF30" strokeWidth="1" />
          <text x="110" y="16" fill="#FFFFFF" fontSize="10" fontFamily="system-ui" fontWeight="700"
            textAnchor="middle" letterSpacing="1">LEGEND</text>
          {/* Storage */}
          <rect x="12" y="28" width="14" height="14" rx="2" fill="#B85C5C" />
          <text x="34" y="36" fill="#DDD" fontSize="10" fontFamily="system-ui" dominantBaseline="middle">
            Lagerplats (klicka för att filtrera)
          </text>
          {/* Production */}
          <rect x="12" y="50" width="14" height="14" rx="2" fill="#7E8E9C" />
          <text x="34" y="58" fill="#DDD" fontSize="10" fontFamily="system-ui" dominantBaseline="middle">
            Produktion / Övrigt
          </text>
          {/* Vegetation */}
          <rect x="12" y="72" width="14" height="14" rx="2" fill="#8EA870" />
          <text x="34" y="80" fill="#DDD" fontSize="10" fontFamily="system-ui" dominantBaseline="middle">
            Vegetation / Mark
          </text>
          {/* Railway */}
          <rect x="12" y="94" width="14" height="14" rx="2" fill="#A0A0A8" />
          <text x="34" y="102" fill="#DDD" fontSize="10" fontFamily="system-ui" dominantBaseline="middle">
            Järnvägsspår
          </text>
        </g>

        {/* ── Title ── */}
        <g transform="translate(10, 10)">
          <rect x="0" y="0" width="270" height="34" rx="6" fill="#1A1A1A90" stroke="#FFFFFF20" strokeWidth="1" />
          <text x="12" y="22" fill="#FFFFFF" fontSize="13" fontFamily="system-ui" fontWeight="700" letterSpacing="0.5">
            {branding.companyName} — Lagerplatskarta
          </text>
        </g>
      </svg>

      {/* ── Hover tooltip (DOM overlay) ── */}
      {hoveredZone && tooltip && hoveredStat && (
        <div
          className="absolute z-50 pointer-events-none bg-gray-900/95 border border-white/20 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 shadow-xl text-white"
          style={{
            left: Math.min(tooltip.x + 12, (tooltip.x > 500 ? tooltip.x - 170 : tooltip.x + 12)),
            top: Math.max(8, tooltip.y - 70),
            minWidth: 160,
          }}
        >
          <div className="font-bold text-sm mb-1">{hoveredStat.namn}</div>
          <div className="text-xs text-gray-300 space-y-0.5">
             {hoveredZoneDef?.detailSlug && inventeringsprogressBySlug?.[hoveredZoneDef.detailSlug] ? (
               <div>
                 {inventeringsprogressBySlug[hoveredZoneDef.detailSlug].klara} / {inventeringsprogressBySlug[hoveredZoneDef.detailSlug].totalt} fack klara
               </div>
             ) : (
               <>
                 <div>{hoveredStat.m3.toFixed(2)} m³ totalt</div>
                 <div>{hoveredStat.count} poster · {hoveredStat.pkt.toLocaleString("sv-SE")} pkt</div>
               </>
             )}
          </div>
           <div className="mt-1.5 text-xs text-blue-300 font-medium">{omgangId ? "Klicka för att inventera" : "Klicka för att filtrera"}</div>
        </div>
      )}
      {hoveredZone && tooltip && !hoveredStat && (
        <div
          className="absolute z-50 pointer-events-none bg-gray-900/95 border border-white/20 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 shadow-xl text-white"
          style={{ left: tooltip.x + 12, top: Math.max(8, tooltip.y - 60), minWidth: 140 }}
        >
          <div className="font-bold text-sm mb-1">
            {hoveredZoneDef?.lines.join(" ")}
          </div>
          <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">Ingen data inlagd</div>
          <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-blue-300 font-medium">Klicka för att filtrera</div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/components/LegoSkannaDialog.tsx ================

import { useState, useRef } from "react";
import { customFetch } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import {
  Camera, Upload, Loader2, CheckCircle2, ScanLine, FileText, FileSpreadsheet, X
} from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Checkbox } from "@/components/ui/checkbox";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle, DialogDescription } from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";

// Vad AI-scan-note returnerar
interface ScannedPackage {
  pktNr: string;
  traslag: string | null;
  profil: string | null;
  kvalitet: string | null;
  nomTjocklek: number | null;
  nomBredd: number | null;
  aktTjocklek: number | null;
  aktBredd: number | null;
  langdDm: number | null;
  antal: number | null;
  m3: number | null;
  mottaget: string | null;
  datumIn: string | null;
  anteckning: string | null;
}

interface ScanResult {
  avsandare: string | null;
  datum: string | null;
  paket: ScannedPackage[];
}

// Vad vi lägger till i lego-pakettabellen
export interface LegoPaketRow {
  pktNr: string;
  traslag: string;
  kvalitet: string;
  rawTjocklek: string;
  rawBredd: string;
  hyvlatTill: string;
  hyvlTjocklek: string;
  hyvlBredd: string;
  langdDm: string;
  antal: string;
  m3: string;
  lpm: string;
  prisM3: string;
  anteckning: string;
}

function mapToLegoPaket(p: ScannedPackage, prisM3: string): LegoPaketRow {
  const langd = p.langdDm ?? 0;
  const ant = p.antal ?? 0;
  const rawTk = p.nomTjocklek ?? 0;
  const rawBr = p.nomBredd ?? 0;
  let m3 = p.m3;
  if (!m3 && rawTk && rawBr && langd && ant) {
    m3 = parseFloat(((rawTk / 1000) * (rawBr / 1000) * (langd / 10) * ant).toFixed(4));
  }
  const lpm = langd && ant ? parseFloat(((langd / 10) * ant).toFixed(2)) : 0;
  return {
    pktNr: p.pktNr ?? "",
    traslag: p.traslag ?? "",
    kvalitet: p.kvalitet ?? "",
    rawTjocklek: p.nomTjocklek?.toString() ?? "",
    rawBredd: p.nomBredd?.toString() ?? "",
    hyvlatTill: p.profil ?? "",
    hyvlTjocklek: p.aktTjocklek?.toString() ?? "",
    hyvlBredd: p.aktBredd?.toString() ?? "",
    langdDm: p.langdDm?.toString() ?? "",
    antal: p.antal?.toString() ?? "",
    m3: m3?.toString() ?? "",
    lpm: lpm?.toString() ?? "",
    prisM3,
    anteckning: p.anteckning ?? "",
  };
}

type FileKind = "image" | "pdf" | "excel" | "heic";
function getFileKind(file: File): FileKind {
  const mime = file.type.toLowerCase();
  const name = file.name.toLowerCase();
  if (mime.includes("spreadsheet") || mime.includes("excel") || mime === "text/csv" ||
    name.endsWith(".xlsx") || name.endsWith(".xls") || name.endsWith(".csv")) return "excel";
  if (mime === "application/pdf" || name.endsWith(".pdf")) return "pdf";
  if (mime === "image/heic" || mime === "image/heif" || name.endsWith(".heic") || name.endsWith(".heif")) return "heic";
  return "image";
}

export interface LegoSkanMeta {
  avsandare: string | null;
  datum: string | null;
}

interface LegoSkannaDialogProps {
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
  onPackagesFound: (paket: LegoPaketRow[], meta: LegoSkanMeta) => void;
  defaultPrisM3?: string;
}

export default function LegoSkannaDialog({ open, onOpenChange, onPackagesFound, defaultPrisM3 = "" }: LegoSkannaDialogProps) {
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [fileKind, setFileKind] = useState<FileKind>("image");
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [scanning, setScanning] = useState(false);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<ScanResult | null>(null);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set());
  const [globalPrisM3, setGlobalPrisM3] = useState<string>(defaultPrisM3);
  const [perPaketPris, setPerPaketPris] = useState<Record<number, string>>({});
  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const cameraRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const { toast } = useToast();

  function reset() {
    setFile(null);
    setFileKind("image");
    setPreview(null);
    setResult(null);
    setSelected(new Set());
    setPerPaketPris({});
  }

  function handleFile(f: File) {
    setFile(f);
    setResult(null);
    setSelected(new Set());
    const kind = getFileKind(f);
    setFileKind(kind);
    if (kind === "image") {
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = (e) => setPreview(e.target?.result as string);
      reader.readAsDataURL(f);
    } else {
      setPreview(null);
    }
  }

  async function handleScan() {
    if (!file) return;
    setScanning(true);
    try {
      const form = new FormData();
      form.append("image", file);
      const data = await customFetch<ScanResult>("/api/scan-note", { method: "POST", body: form });
      setResult(data);
      setSelected(new Set(data.paket.map((_, i) => i)));
      setPerPaketPris({});
    } catch (e: any) {
      toast({ title: "Skanningsfel", description: e.message ?? "Kunde inte läsa dokumentet.", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setScanning(false);
    }
  }

  function getPrisForIndex(i: number): string {
    return perPaketPris[i] !== undefined ? perPaketPris[i] : globalPrisM3;
  }

  function confirm() {
    if (!result) return;
    const rows = result.paket
      .filter((_, i) => selected.has(i))
      .map((p, origIdx) => {
        const actualIdx = result.paket.indexOf(p);
        return mapToLegoPaket(p, getPrisForIndex(actualIdx));
      });
    onPackagesFound(rows, { avsandare: result.avsandare, datum: result.datum });
    onOpenChange(false);
    reset();
    toast({ title: `${rows.length} paket lades till från skanningen` });
  }

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={(v) => { onOpenChange(v); if (!v) reset(); }}>
      <DialogContent className="max-w-2xl max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <ScanLine className="h-5 w-5" />
            Skanna lego-följesedel
          </DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>
            Ladda upp ett foto, PDF eller Excel av kundens följesedel. AI läser in paketen automatiskt.
            Råvara → <em>Nom dim</em>, Färdig dim → <em>Akt dim</em>.
          </DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>

        {!result && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {!file ? (
              <div className="border-2 border-dashed rounded-lg p-10 text-center space-y-4">
                <ScanLine className="h-12 w-12 mx-auto text-muted-foreground" />
                <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Välj ett foto, PDF eller Excel av följesedeln</p>
                <div className="flex gap-3 justify-center flex-wrap">
                  <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => cameraRef.current?.click()}>
                    <Camera className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Ta foto
                  </Button>
                  <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()}>
                    <Upload className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Välj fil
                  </Button>
                </div>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  Stödda format: JPG, PNG, HEIC, WebP · PDF · Excel (xlsx, xls, csv)
                </p>
                <input ref={cameraRef} type="file" accept="image/*" capture="environment" className="hidden"
                  onChange={(e) => e.target.files?.[0] && handleFile(e.target.files[0])} />
                <input ref={fileRef} type="file"
                  accept="image/*,application/pdf,.pdf,.xlsx,.xls,.csv,text/csv,application/vnd.ms-excel,application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet"
                  className="hidden"
                  onChange={(e) => e.target.files?.[0] && handleFile(e.target.files[0])} />
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <div className="relative rounded-lg overflow-hidden border">
                  {fileKind === "image" && preview ? (
                    <img src={preview} alt="Följesedel" className="w-full max-h-72 object-contain bg-gray-50" />
                  ) : (
                    <div className="w-full h-40 bg-gray-50 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 text-muted-foreground px-4">
                      {fileKind === "pdf" ? <FileText className="h-10 w-10" /> : <FileSpreadsheet className="h-10 w-10" />}
                      <span className="text-sm font-medium text-center break-all">{file.name}</span>
                      <Badge variant="secondary" className="text-xs">
                        {fileKind === "pdf" ? "PDF" : fileKind === "excel" ? "Excel/CSV" : "HEIC"}
                        {fileKind === "heic" && " — konverteras automatiskt"}
                      </Badge>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <Button size="sm" variant="outline" className="absolute top-2 right-2" onClick={reset}>
                    <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5 mr-1" /> Byt fil
                  </Button>
                </div>
                <Button className="w-full" onClick={handleScan} disabled={scanning}>
                  {scanning
                    ? <><Loader2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> AI analyserar...</>
                    : <><ScanLine className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Analysera {fileKind === "excel" ? "Excel-fil" : fileKind === "pdf" ? "PDF" : "följesedel"}</>}
                </Button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {result && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {(result.avsandare || result.datum) && (
              <div className="bg-muted/40 rounded-lg p-3 text-sm flex gap-4 flex-wrap">
                {result.avsandare && <span><span className="font-medium">Avsändare:</span> {result.avsandare}</span>}
                {result.datum && <span><span className="font-medium">Datum:</span> {result.datum}</span>}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Global price input */}
            <div className="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-lg p-3 space-y-2">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs font-medium text-amber-900">Pris/m³ (alla paket)</Label>
                  <Input
                    className="h-8 mt-1 text-sm"
                    value={globalPrisM3}
                    onChange={e => setGlobalPrisM3(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="kr/m³ — lämna tomt om ej känt"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-amber-700">Sätts på alla paket. Kan justeras per paket nedan eller på jobbet efteråt.</p>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <p className="text-sm font-medium">{result.paket.length} paket hittades</p>
              <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => {
                if (selected.size === result.paket.length) setSelected(new Set());
                else setSelected(new Set(result.paket.map((_, i) => i)));
              }}>
                {selected.size === result.paket.length ? "Avmarkera alla" : "Markera alla"}
              </Button>
            </div>

            <div className="border rounded-lg divide-y max-h-72 overflow-y-auto">
              {result.paket.map((pkg, i) => {
                const mapped = mapToLegoPaket(pkg, "");
                const paketPris = perPaketPris[i] !== undefined ? perPaketPris[i] : globalPrisM3;
                return (
                  <div key={i} className="px-4 py-3">
                    <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                      <Checkbox className="mt-0.5" checked={selected.has(i)}
                        onCheckedChange={(v) => {
                          const s = new Set(selected);
                          if (v) s.add(i); else s.delete(i);
                          setSelected(s);
                        }} />
                      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 space-y-1.5">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                          <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-sm">{pkg.pktNr || "–"}</span>
                          {pkg.traslag && <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs">{pkg.traslag}</Badge>}
                          {pkg.kvalitet && <Badge variant="secondary" className="text-xs">{pkg.kvalitet}</Badge>}
                          {pkg.profil && <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs">{pkg.profil}</Badge>}
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex gap-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground flex-wrap">
                          {mapped.rawTjocklek && mapped.rawBredd && (
                            <span>Råvara {mapped.rawTjocklek}×{mapped.rawBredd} mm</span>
                          )}
                          {mapped.hyvlTjocklek && mapped.hyvlBredd && (
                            <span>Hyvlad {mapped.hyvlTjocklek}×{mapped.hyvlBredd} mm</span>
                          )}
                          {mapped.langdDm && <span>{mapped.langdDm} dm</span>}
                          {mapped.antal && <span>{mapped.antal} st</span>}
                          {mapped.m3 && <span>{parseFloat(mapped.m3).toFixed(3)} m³</span>}
                        </div>
                        {selected.has(i) && (
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                            <Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground whitespace-nowrap">Pris/m³:</Label>
                            <Input
                              className="h-7 w-28 text-xs"
                              value={paketPris}
                              onChange={e => setPerPaketPris(prev => ({ ...prev, [i]: e.target.value }))}
                              placeholder={globalPrisM3 || "kr/m³"}
                            />
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                      {selected.has(i) && <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 text-green-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>

            <div className="flex gap-3">
              <Button variant="outline" onClick={reset} className="flex-1">
                Skanna ny
              </Button>
              <Button className="flex-1" onClick={confirm} disabled={selected.size === 0}>
                Lägg till {selected.size} paket
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}

export interface ScanResult {

export interface ScannedPackage {
  pktNr: string;
  traslag: string | null;

  nomBredd: number | null;

type LagerOmradeSummary = {
  id: number;
  namn: string;
  slug: string;
  fackAntal: number;
};

type LagerOmradeDetalj = {
  id: number;
  fack: Array<{ id: number; kod: string }>;
};

interface ScanDialogProps {
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
  /** Om angiven: visas "Skapa spec"-knapp och paketen skickas hit istället för att importeras till lagret */
  onPackagesConfirmed?: (result: ScanResult) => Promise<void>;
}

type FileKind = "image" | "pdf" | "excel" | "heic";

function getFileKind(file: File): FileKind {
  const mime = file.type.toLowerCase();
  const name = file.name.toLowerCase();
  if (mime.includes("spreadsheet") || mime.includes("excel") || mime === "text/csv" ||
      name.endsWith(".xlsx") || name.endsWith(".xls") || name.endsWith(".csv")) return "excel";
  if (mime === "application/pdf" || name.endsWith(".pdf")) return "pdf";
  if (mime === "image/heic" || mime === "image/heif" || name.endsWith(".heic") || name.endsWith(".heif")) return "heic";
  return "image";
}

function dimLabel(pkg: ScannedPackage): string {
  const parts: string[] = [];
  if (pkg.nomTjocklek && pkg.nomBredd) parts.push(`${pkg.nomTjocklek}×${pkg.nomBredd}mm`);
  if (pkg.langdDm) parts.push(`${pkg.langdDm}dm`);
  if (pkg.antal) parts.push(`${pkg.antal}st`);
  return parts.join(" · ");
}

function FileIcon({ kind, className }: { kind: FileKind; className?: string }) {
  if (kind === "pdf") return <FileText className={className} />;
  if (kind === "excel") return <FileSpreadsheet className={className} />;
  return <Camera className={className} />;
}

function fileBadgeLabel(kind: FileKind): string {
  if (kind === "pdf") return "PDF";
  if (kind === "excel") return "Excel/CSV";
  if (kind === "heic") return "HEIC";
  return "Bild";
}

export function ScanDialog({ open, onOpenChange, onPackagesConfirmed }: ScanDialogProps) {
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [fileKind, setFileKind] = useState<FileKind>("image");
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [scanning, setScanning] = useState(false);
  const [importing, setImporting] = useState(false);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<ScanResult | null>(null);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set());
  const [done, setDone] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [updated, setUpdated] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [existingPktNrs, setExistingPktNrs] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [avrapporteradePktNrs, setAvrapporteradePktNrs] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [m3PrisInput, setM3PrisInput] = useState("");
  const [defaultPlacement, setDefaultPlacement] = useState<ScanImportPlacement>({ omradeId: null, fackId: null });
  const [distributePlacements, setDistributePlacements] = useState(false);
  const [packagePlacements, setPackagePlacements] = useState<Record<number, ScanImportPlacement>>({});
  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const cameraRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const { data: omraden = [] } = useQuery<LagerOmradeSummary[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/lager/omraden"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<LagerOmradeSummary[]>("/api/lager/omraden"),
    staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000,
  });
  const { data: fackByOmrade = {} } = useQuery<Record<number, Array<{ id: number; kod: string }>>>({
    queryKey: ["/api/lager/omraden", "alla-fack", omraden.map((o) => o.id).join(",")],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const details = await Promise.all(
        omraden.map((omrade) =>
          customFetch<LagerOmradeDetalj>(`/api/lager/omraden/${omrade.slug}`),
        ),
      );
      return Object.fromEntries(details.map((detail) => [detail.id, detail.fack]));
    },
    enabled: omraden.length > 0,
    staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000,
  });

  function reset() {
    setFile(null);
    setFileKind("image");
    setPreview(null);
    setResult(null);
    setSelected(new Set());
    setDone([]);
    setUpdated([]);
    setExistingPktNrs(new Set());
    setAvrapporteradePktNrs(new Set());
    setM3PrisInput("");
    setDefaultPlacement({ omradeId: null, fackId: null });
    setDistributePlacements(false);
    setPackagePlacements({});
  }

  function handleFile(f: File) {
    setFile(f);
    setResult(null);
    setSelected(new Set());
    setDone([]);

    const kind = getFileKind(f);
    setFileKind(kind);

    if (kind === "image") {
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = (e) => setPreview(e.target?.result as string);
      reader.readAsDataURL(f);
    } else {
      setPreview(null);
    }
  }

  async function handleScan() {
    if (!file) return;
    setScanning(true);
    const abortCtrl = new AbortController();
    const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => abortCtrl.abort(), 90_000);
    try {
      const form = new FormData();
      form.append("image", file);
      const data = await customFetch<ScanResult>("/api/scan-note", {
        method: "POST",
        body: form,
        signal: abortCtrl.signal,
      });
      clearTimeout(timeoutId);
      setResult(data);
      setSelected(new Set(data.paket.map((_, i) => i)));

      const pktNrs = data.paket.map(p => p.pktNr).filter(Boolean) as string[];
      if (pktNrs.length > 0) {
        try {
          const { existing, avrapporterade } = await customFetch<{ existing: string[]; avrapporterade: string[] }>("/api/lager/check-duplicates", {
            method: "POST",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
            body: JSON.stringify({ pktNrs }),
          });
          setExistingPktNrs(new Set(existing));
          setAvrapporteradePktNrs(new Set(avrapporterade));
        } catch { /* ignorera duplikat-fel */ }
      }
    } catch (e: any) {
      clearTimeout(timeoutId);
      const isTimeout = e?.name === "AbortError" || (e?.message ?? "").toLowerCase().includes("timeout");
      toast({
        title: "Skanningsfel",
        description: isTimeout
          ? "AI-tjänsten svarade inte i tid. Försök igen."
          : e.message,
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    } finally {
      setScanning(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleImport() {
    if (!result) return;
    setImporting(true);
    const m3Pris = m3PrisInput.trim() ? parseFloat(m3PrisInput.replace(",", ".")) : null;
    const validPris = m3Pris && !isNaN(m3Pris) && m3Pris > 0 ? m3Pris : null;

    // Om onPackagesConfirmed är angiven: skicka valda paket till callback (skapar spec, ej lager)
    if (onPackagesConfirmed) {
      const selectedPaket = result.paket.flatMap((pkg, i) => {
        if (!selected.has(i)) return [];
        const placement = effectivePackagePlacement(
          distributePlacements,
          defaultPlacement,
          packagePlacements,
          i,
        );
        const nomM3 = (pkg.nomTjocklek && pkg.nomBredd && pkg.langdDm && pkg.antal)
          ? parseFloat(((pkg.nomTjocklek / 1000) * (pkg.nomBredd / 1000) * (pkg.langdDm / 10) * pkg.antal).toFixed(4))
          : pkg.m3;
        return [{
          ...pkg,
          ...placement,
          m3Pris: validPris,
          m3Sum: validPris && nomM3 ? parseFloat((validPris * nomM3).toFixed(2)) : null,
        }];
      });
      const filteredResult: ScanResult = { ...result, paket: selectedPaket };
      try {
        await onPackagesConfirmed(filteredResult);
        onOpenChange(false);
        reset();
      } catch (e: any) {
        toast({ title: "Fel vid skapande av spec", description: e.message, variant: "destructive" });
      }
      setImporting(false);
      return;
    }

    const newDone: number[] = [...done];
    const newUpdated: number[] = [...updated];

    for (let i = 0; i < result.paket.length; i++) {
      if (!selected.has(i)) continue;
      const pkg = result.paket[i];
      const placement = effectivePackagePlacement(
        distributePlacements,
        defaultPlacement,
        packagePlacements,
        i,
      );
      try {
        const datumIn = pkg.datumIn || result.datum || format(new Date(), "yyyy-MM-dd");
        const mottaget = pkg.mottaget || result.avsandare || null;

        let m3 = pkg.m3;
        if (!m3 && pkg.langdDm && pkg.antal) {
          const tk = pkg.aktTjocklek ?? pkg.nomTjocklek;
          const br = pkg.aktBredd ?? pkg.nomBredd;
          if (tk && br) {
            m3 = parseFloat(((tk / 1000) * (br / 1000) * (pkg.langdDm / 10) * pkg.antal).toFixed(4));
          }
        }

        // Pris beräknas alltid på Nom m3, aldrig på akt m3
        const nomM3 = (pkg.nomTjocklek && pkg.nomBredd && pkg.langdDm && pkg.antal)
          ? parseFloat(((pkg.nomTjocklek / 1000) * (pkg.nomBredd / 1000) * (pkg.langdDm / 10) * pkg.antal).toFixed(4))
          : m3;
        const m3Sum = validPris && nomM3 ? parseFloat((validPris * nomM3).toFixed(2)) : null;

        const body = {
          pktNr: pkg.pktNr,
          traslag: pkg.traslag,
          profil: pkg.profil,
          kvalitet: pkg.kvalitet,
          nomTjocklek: pkg.nomTjocklek,
          nomBredd: pkg.nomBredd,
          actTjocklek: pkg.aktTjocklek,
          actBredd: pkg.aktBredd,
          langdDm: pkg.langdDm,
          antal: pkg.antal,
          m3,
          lpm: pkg.langdDm && pkg.antal ? parseFloat(((pkg.langdDm / 10) * pkg.antal).toFixed(2)) : null,
          mottaget,
          datumIn,
          anteckning: pkg.anteckning,
          m3Pris: validPris,
          m3Sum,
          leverantor: result.avsandare || null,
          omradeId: placement.omradeId,
          fackId: placement.fackId,
        };

        const data = await customFetch<{ _action?: string }>("/api/lager/upsert", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify(body),
        });
        newDone.push(i);
        if (data._action === "updated") newUpdated.push(i);
        setDone([...newDone]);
        setUpdated([...newUpdated]);
      } catch {
        toast({ title: `Fel vid import av paket ${pkg.pktNr}`, variant: "destructive" });
      }
    }

    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager"] });
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager/statistik"] });
    setImporting(false);

    const total = result.paket.filter((_, i) => selected.has(i)).length;
    const nySkapade = newDone.length - newUpdated.length;
    const uppdaterade = newUpdated.length;
    const delar = [];
    if (nySkapade > 0) delar.push(`${nySkapade} nya`);
    if (uppdaterade > 0) delar.push(`${uppdaterade} uppdaterade`);
    toast({
      title: `${newDone.length} av ${total} paket importerade`,
      description: delar.join(", "),
    });
    if (newDone.length === total) {
      setTimeout(() => { onOpenChange(false); reset(); }, 1500);
    }
  }

  function toggleAll() {
    if (result) {
      if (selected.size === result.paket.length) setSelected(new Set());
      else setSelected(new Set(result.paket.map((_, i) => i)));
    }
  }

  const hasFile = file !== null;
  const defaultFack = filterFackForArea(fackByOmrade, defaultPlacement.omradeId);
  const defaultOmrade = omraden.find((omrade) => omrade.id === defaultPlacement.omradeId);
  const defaultFackLabel = defaultFack.find((fack) => fack.id === defaultPlacement.fackId)?.kod;
  const importLocationLabel = defaultOmrade
    ? `${defaultOmrade.namn}${defaultFackLabel ? ` · ${defaultFackLabel}` : ""}`
    : "utan lagerplats";

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={(v) => { onOpenChange(v); if (!v) reset(); }}>
      <DialogContent className="max-w-2xl max-h-[90dvh] overflow-y-auto">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <ScanLine className="h-5 w-5" />
            Scanna dokument
          </DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>
            Ladda upp en bild, PDF eller Excel-fil med följesedel eller spec. AI:n plockar ut paketnummer, dimensioner, antal, längd och kvalitet automatiskt.
          </DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>

        {!result && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {!hasFile ? (
              <div className="border-2 border-dashed rounded-lg p-10 text-center space-y-4">
                <ScanLine className="h-12 w-12 mx-auto text-muted-foreground" />
                <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                  Välj ett foto, PDF eller Excel-fil av följesedeln
                </p>
                <div className="flex gap-3 justify-center flex-wrap">
                  <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => cameraRef.current?.click()}>
                    <Camera className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Ta foto
                  </Button>
                  <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()}>
                    <Upload className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Välj fil
                  </Button>
                </div>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  Stödda format: JPG, PNG, HEIC, WebP · PDF · Excel (xlsx, xls, csv)
                </p>
                <input
                  ref={cameraRef}
                  type="file"
                  accept="image/*"
                  capture="environment"
                  className="hidden"
                  onChange={(e) => e.target.files?.[0] && handleFile(e.target.files[0])}
                />
                <input
                  ref={fileRef}
                  type="file"
                  accept="image/*,application/pdf,.pdf,.xlsx,.xls,.csv,text/csv,application/vnd.ms-excel,application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet"
                  className="hidden"
                  onChange={(e) => e.target.files?.[0] && handleFile(e.target.files[0])}
                />
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <div className="relative rounded-lg overflow-hidden border">
                  {fileKind === "image" && preview ? (
                    <img src={preview} alt="Följesedel" className="w-full max-h-72 object-contain bg-gray-50" />
                  ) : (
                    <div className="w-full h-40 bg-gray-50 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 text-muted-foreground px-4">
                      <FileIcon kind={fileKind} className="h-10 w-10" />
                      <span className="text-sm font-medium text-center break-all">{file?.name}</span>
                      <Badge variant="secondary" className="text-xs">
                        {fileBadgeLabel(fileKind)}
                        {fileKind === "heic" && " — konverteras automatiskt"}
                      </Badge>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <Button size="sm" variant="outline" className="absolute top-2 right-2" onClick={reset}>
                    Byt fil
                  </Button>
                </div>
                <Button className="w-full" onClick={handleScan} disabled={scanning}>
                  {scanning
                    ? <><Loader2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> AI analyserar...</>
                    : <><ScanLine className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Analysera {fileKind === "excel" ? "Excel-fil" : fileKind === "pdf" ? "PDF" : "följesedel"}</>}
                </Button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {result && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {(result.avsandare || result.datum) && (
              <div className="bg-muted/40 rounded-lg p-3 text-sm flex gap-4 flex-wrap">
                {result.avsandare && <span><span className="font-medium">Avsändare:</span> {result.avsandare}</span>}
                {result.datum && <span><span className="font-medium">Datum:</span> {result.datum}</span>}
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <p className="text-sm font-medium">{result.paket.length} paket hittades</p>
              <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={toggleAll}>
                {selected.size === result.paket.length ? "Avmarkera alla" : "Markera alla"}
              </Button>
            </div>

            <div className="border rounded-lg divide-y">
              {result.paket.map((pkg, i) => {
                const isDuplicate = pkg.pktNr ? existingPktNrs.has(pkg.pktNr) : false;
                const isAvrap = pkg.pktNr ? avrapporteradePktNrs.has(pkg.pktNr) : false;
                const rowBg = done.includes(i) ? "bg-green-50" : isAvrap ? "bg-red-50" : isDuplicate ? "bg-amber-50" : "";
                return (
                  <div
                    key={i}
                    className={`px-4 py-3 ${rowBg} ${done.includes(i) ? "" : "cursor-pointer"}`}
                    onClick={() => {
                      if (done.includes(i)) return;
                      const next = new Set(selected);
                      if (next.has(i)) next.delete(i); else next.add(i);
                      setSelected(next);
                    }}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                      {done.includes(i)
                        ? <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 text-green-600 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                        : <Checkbox className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5" checked={selected.has(i)} onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()} onCheckedChange={(v) => {
                            const s = new Set(selected);
                            if (v) s.add(i); else s.delete(i);
                            setSelected(s);
                          }} />
                      }
                      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 space-y-1">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                          <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-sm">{pkg.pktNr}</span>
                          {isAvrap && (
                            <Badge className="text-xs bg-red-100 text-red-800 border-red-300 hover:bg-red-100">
                              Avrapporterad
                            </Badge>
                          )}
                          {!isAvrap && isDuplicate && (
                            <Badge className="text-xs bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border-amber-300 hover:bg-amber-100">
                              Finns redan
                            </Badge>
                          )}
                          {pkg.traslag && <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs">{pkg.traslag}</Badge>}
                          {pkg.profil && <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs">{pkg.profil}</Badge>}
                          {pkg.kvalitet && <Badge variant="secondary" className="text-xs">{pkg.kvalitet}</Badge>}
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex gap-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground flex-wrap">
                          {pkg.nomTjocklek && pkg.nomBredd && (
                            <span>Dim: {pkg.nomTjocklek}×{pkg.nomBredd} mm
                              {pkg.aktTjocklek && pkg.aktBredd && (
                                <span className="text-muted-foreground/70"> ({pkg.aktTjocklek}×{pkg.aktBredd})</span>
                              )}
                            </span>
                          )}
                          {pkg.langdDm && <span>Längd: {pkg.langdDm} dm</span>}
                          {pkg.antal && <span>Antal: {pkg.antal} st</span>}
                          {pkg.m3 && <span>Volym: {pkg.m3} m³</span>}
                        </div>
                        {isAvrap && !done.includes(i) && (
                          <p className="text-xs text-red-700">Paketet är avrapporterat — förblir avrapporterat om du importerar.</p>
                        )}
                        {!isAvrap && isDuplicate && !done.includes(i) && (
                          <p className="text-xs text-amber-700">Paket finns redan i lagret — uppdaterar befintlig post.</p>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>

            <div className="rounded-lg border bg-muted/30 p-3 space-y-3">
              <div className="space-y-3">
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                  <div className="space-y-1.5">
                    <Label className="text-sm font-medium">Lagerområde</Label>
                    <Select
                      value={defaultPlacement.omradeId == null ? "none" : String(defaultPlacement.omradeId)}
                      onValueChange={(value) => {
                        const next = placementAfterAreaChange(value === "none" ? null : Number(value));
                        setDefaultPlacement(next);
                      }}
                      disabled={importing}
                    >
                      <SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
                      <SelectContent>
                        <SelectItem value="none">Ingen lagerplats ännu</SelectItem>
                        {omraden.map((omrade) => (
                          <SelectItem key={omrade.id} value={String(omrade.id)}>{omrade.namn}</SelectItem>
                        ))}
                      </SelectContent>
                    </Select>
                  </div>
                  <div className="space-y-1.5">
                    <Label className="text-sm font-medium">Fack</Label>
                    <Select
                      value={defaultPlacement.fackId == null ? "none" : String(defaultPlacement.fackId)}
                      onValueChange={(value) => setDefaultPlacement((current) => ({
                        ...current,
                        fackId: value === "none" ? null : Number(value),
                      }))}
                      disabled={importing || defaultPlacement.omradeId == null || defaultFack.length === 0}
                    >
                      <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder="Inget fack" /></SelectTrigger>
                      <SelectContent>
                        <SelectItem value="none">Inget fack</SelectItem>
                        {defaultFack.map((fack) => (
                          <SelectItem key={fack.id} value={String(fack.id)}>{fack.kod}</SelectItem>
                        ))}
                      </SelectContent>
                    </Select>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Gäller alla markerade paket i lasset</p>
                <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium">
                  <Checkbox
                    checked={distributePlacements}
                    onCheckedChange={(checked) => {
                      const enabled = checked === true;
                      setDistributePlacements(enabled);
                      setPackagePlacements(enabled ? copyDefaultPlacement(selected, defaultPlacement) : {});
                    }}
                    disabled={importing}
                  />
                  Fördela paket på olika platser
                </label>
                {distributePlacements && (
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    {result.paket.map((pkg, i) => {
                      if (!selected.has(i)) return null;
                      const placement = packagePlacements[i] ?? defaultPlacement;
                      const fack = filterFackForArea(fackByOmrade, placement.omradeId);
                      return (
                        <details key={`${pkg.pktNr}-${i}`} className="group rounded-md border bg-background">
                          <summary className="flex min-h-11 cursor-pointer list-none items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium [&::-webkit-details-marker]:hidden">
                            <ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 transition-transform group-open:rotate-90" />
                            <span className="min-w-0">
                              Paket {pkg.pktNr || i + 1}
                              <span className="ml-2 font-normal text-muted-foreground">
                                {[pkg.traslag, pkg.profil, dimLabel(pkg)].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")}
                              </span>
                            </span>
                          </summary>
                          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 border-t px-3 py-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                            <Select
                              value={placement.omradeId == null ? "none" : String(placement.omradeId)}
                              onValueChange={(value) => setPackagePlacements((current) => ({
                                ...current,
                                [i]: placementAfterAreaChange(value === "none" ? null : Number(value)),
                              }))}
                              disabled={importing}
                            >
                              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
                              <SelectContent>
                                <SelectItem value="none">Ingen lagerplats ännu</SelectItem>
                                {omraden.map((omrade) => (
                                  <SelectItem key={omrade.id} value={String(omrade.id)}>{omrade.namn}</SelectItem>
                                ))}
                              </SelectContent>
                            </Select>
                            <Select
                              value={placement.fackId == null ? "none" : String(placement.fackId)}
                              onValueChange={(value) => setPackagePlacements((current) => ({
                                ...current,
                                [i]: { ...placement, fackId: value === "none" ? null : Number(value) },
                              }))}
                              disabled={importing || placement.omradeId == null || fack.length === 0}
                            >
                              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder="Inget fack" /></SelectTrigger>
                              <SelectContent>
                                <SelectItem value="none">Inget fack</SelectItem>
                                {fack.map((item) => (
                                  <SelectItem key={item.id} value={String(item.id)}>{item.kod}</SelectItem>
                                ))}
                              </SelectContent>
                            </Select>
                          </div>
                        </details>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <Label htmlFor="m3pris-input" className="text-sm font-medium">
                  Inköpspris per m³ (kr) — valfritt
                </Label>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Input
                    id="m3pris-input"
                    type="number"
                    min="0"
                    step="0.01"
                    placeholder="t.ex. 1250"
                    value={m3PrisInput}
                    onChange={(e) => setM3PrisInput(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-40"
                    disabled={importing}
                  />
                  <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                    {m3PrisInput && !isNaN(parseFloat(m3PrisInput.replace(",", "."))) && parseFloat(m3PrisInput.replace(",", ".")) > 0
                      ? `Totalt ≈ ${result?.paket.filter((_, i) => selected.has(i)).reduce((sum, p) => sum + (p.m3 ?? 0), 0).toFixed(1)} m³`
                      : "Lämna tomt om inget pris finns på spec"}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 sm:flex-row">
              <Button variant="outline" onClick={reset} className="flex-1" disabled={importing}>
                Scanna ny
              </Button>
              <Button className="flex-1" onClick={handleImport} disabled={importing || selected.size === 0}>
                {importing
                  ? <><Loader2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> {onPackagesConfirmed ? "Skapar spec..." : "Importerar..."}</>
                  : onPackagesConfirmed
                    ? `Skapa spec med ${selected.size} paket ${distributePlacements ? "till valda platser" : `till ${importLocationLabel}`}`
                    : `Importera ${selected.size} paket ${distributePlacements ? "till valda platser" : `till ${importLocationLabel}`}`}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/components/SkannaDialog.tsx ================

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { customFetch } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import { Camera, Loader2, CheckCircle2, ScanLine } from "lucide-react";
import KameraSkanner, { type KameraScanResult } from "@/pages/inventering/KameraSkanner";

type Pkg = {
  id: number;
  pktNr: string | null;
  varuslag: string | null;
  profil: string | null;
  nomTjocklek: number | null;
  nomBredd: number | null;
  plats: string | null;
  [key: string]: unknown;
};

interface SkannaDialogProps {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onFound: (pkg: Pkg) => void;
  onPktNrFunnet?: (pktNr: string) => void | KameraScanResult | Promise<void | KameraScanResult>;
  onOklarKod?: (raValde: string, kodTyp: string) => void;
  continuous?: boolean;
  selectedCount?: number;
}

export default function SkannaDialog({
  open,
  onClose,
  onFound,
  onPktNrFunnet,
  onOklarKod,
  continuous = false,
  selectedCount = 0,
}: SkannaDialogProps) {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const [lage, setLage] = useState<"val" | "kamera" | "ai">("val");

  useEffect(() => {
    if (open) setLage("val");
  }, [open]);

  async function handlePktNrFunnet(pktNr: string) {
    if (onPktNrFunnet) {
      const resultat = await onPktNrFunnet(pktNr);
      if (!continuous) onClose();
      return resultat;
    }

    try {
      const pkg = await customFetch<Pkg>(`/api/lager/by-pktnr/${encodeURIComponent(pktNr)}`);
      onFound(pkg);
      onClose();
    } catch {
      toast({ title: `PKT-NR ${pktNr} hittades inte`, variant: "destructive" });
    }
  }

  function handleOklarKod(raValde: string, kodTyp: string) {
    if (onOklarKod) {
      onOklarKod(raValde, kodTyp);
      if (!continuous) onClose();
      return;
    }
    toast({
      title: `Oklar kod (${kodTyp})`,
      description: `"${raValde}" kunde inte matchas säkert.`,
      variant: "destructive",
    });
  }

  if (open && lage === "kamera") {
    return (
      <KameraSkanner
        onPktNrFunnet={handlePktNrFunnet}
        onOklarKod={handleOklarKod}
        onClose={onClose}
        loggKalla="spec-kamera"
        continuous={continuous}
        selectedCount={selectedCount}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={v => !v && onClose()}>
      <DialogContent className="max-w-md">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>Skanna paketlapp</DialogTitle>
        </DialogHeader>
        {lage === "val" ? (
          <div className="grid gap-3">
            <Button size="lg" className="h-16 justify-start gap-3" onClick={() => setLage("kamera")}>
              <ScanLine className="h-5 w-5" />
              <span className="text-left">
                <span className="block">Skanna streckkod / QR</span>
                <span className="block text-xs font-normal opacity-80">Läs paketets kod med kameran</span>
              </span>
            </Button>
            <Button size="lg" variant="outline" className="h-16 justify-start gap-3" onClick={() => setLage("ai")}>
              <Camera className="h-5 w-5" />
              <span className="text-left">
                <span className="block">Läs paketlapp med AI</span>
                <span className="block text-xs font-normal text-muted-foreground">Ta eller ladda upp ett foto</span>
              </span>
            </Button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => setLage("val")}>Tillbaka till val</Button>
            <AiScanner onFound={onFound} onClose={onClose} />
          </div>
        )}
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}

/* ── AI-fotoskanner ─────────────────────────────────────── */

function AiScanner({ onFound, onClose }: { onFound: (p: Pkg) => void; onClose: () => void }) {
  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [found, setFound] = useState<Pkg | null>(null);
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function handleFile(file: File) {
    setLoading(true);
    setPreview(URL.createObjectURL(file));

    try {
      // Steg 1: AI läser PKT-NR från bilden
      const form = new FormData();
      form.append("image", file);
      let scanData: { pktNr?: string; error?: string };
      try {
        scanData = await customFetch<{ pktNr?: string; error?: string }>("/api/scan-paketlapp", { method: "POST", body: form });
      } catch (e: unknown) {
        toast({
          title: "Kunde inte läsa PKT-NR",
          description: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Försök ta en tydligare bild av paketnumret.",
          variant: "destructive",
        });
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      if (!scanData.pktNr) {
        toast({
          title: "Kunde inte läsa PKT-NR",
          description: scanData.error ?? "Försök ta en tydligare bild av paketnumret.",
          variant: "destructive",
        });
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      // Steg 2: Slå upp paketet
      let foundPkg: Pkg | null = null;
      try {
        foundPkg = await customFetch<Pkg>(`/api/lager/by-pktnr/${encodeURIComponent(scanData.pktNr)}`);
      } catch {
        toast({
          title: `PKT-NR ${scanData.pktNr} hittades inte`,
          description: "Paketet kanske redan är avrapporterat eller saknas i lagret.",
          variant: "destructive",
        });
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      if (foundPkg) setFound(foundPkg);
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Något gick fel", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  if (found) {
    return (
      <FoundCard pkg={found} onConfirm={() => { onFound(found); onClose(); }} onCancel={() => { setFound(null); setPreview(null); }} />
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {preview && (
        <div className="rounded-lg overflow-hidden border aspect-video relative bg-muted">
          <img src={preview} alt="Förhandsgranskning" className="w-full h-full object-contain" />
          {loading && (
            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/60 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2">
              <Loader2 className="h-8 w-8 text-white animate-spin" />
              <span className="text-white text-sm">AI läser PKT-NR…</span>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      <input
        ref={fileRef}
        type="file"
        accept="image/*"
        capture="environment"
        className="hidden"
        onChange={e => {
          const file = e.target.files?.[0];
          if (file) handleFile(file);
          e.target.value = "";
        }}
      />

      <Button
        className="w-full gap-2"
        size="lg"
        disabled={loading}
        onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()}
      >
        {loading ? (
          <><Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> Analyserar…</>
        ) : (
          <><Camera className="h-4 w-4" /> Fotografera paketlapp</>
        )}
      </Button>

      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground text-center">
        Ta en tydlig bild av paketnumret (PKT-NR) på paketlappen.
        AI:n läser av numret och hittar paketet i systemet.
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ── Hittad-kort ────────────────────────────────────────── */

function FoundCard({ pkg, onConfirm, onCancel }: { pkg: Pkg; onConfirm: () => void; onCancel: () => void }) {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="rounded-lg border bg-green-50 dark:bg-green-950/20 p-4 space-y-2">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-green-700 dark:text-green-400 font-semibold">
          <CheckCircle2 className="h-5 w-5" />
          Paket hittat!
        </div>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-sm">
          <span className="text-muted-foreground">PKT-NR</span>
          <span className="font-bold">{pkg.pktNr ?? `ID:${pkg.id}`}</span>
          <span className="text-muted-foreground">Varuslag</span>
          <span>{pkg.varuslag ?? "—"}</span>
          <span className="text-muted-foreground">Profil</span>
          <span>{pkg.profil ?? "—"}</span>
          <span className="text-muted-foreground">Dimension</span>
          <span>{pkg.nomTjocklek && pkg.nomBredd ? `${pkg.nomTjocklek}×${pkg.nomBredd}` : "—"}</span>
          <span className="text-muted-foreground">Lagerplats</span>
          <span>{pkg.plats ?? "—"}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <Button variant="outline" className="flex-1" onClick={onCancel}>Avbryt</Button>
        <Button className="flex-1 gap-2" onClick={onConfirm}>
          <ScanLine className="h-4 w-4" /> Lägg till i spec
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useId, forwardRef } from "react";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";

interface SuggestInputProps extends React.InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> {
  suggestions: string[];
}

const SuggestInput = forwardRef<HTMLInputElement, SuggestInputProps>(
  ({ suggestions, className, ...props }, ref) => {
    const listId = useId();

    return (
      <>
        <Input
          ref={ref}
          list={listId}
          autoComplete="off"
          className={cn(className)}
          {...props}
        />
        <datalist id={listId}>
          {suggestions.map((s) => (
            <option key={s} value={s} />
          ))}
        </datalist>
      </>
    );
  }
);

SuggestInput.displayName = "SuggestInput";
export { SuggestInput };


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/components/branding/BrandingContext.tsx ================

import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { customFetch } from "@workspace/api-client-react";

export type Branding = {
  companyName: string;
  logoPath: string | null;
  logoUrl: string | null;
  streetAddress: string;
  postalCode: string;
  city: string;
  country: string;
  phone: string;
  email: string;
  organizationNumber: string;
  vatNumber: string;
  website: string;
  certificationText: string;
  certificationNumber: string;
  invoiceDetails: string;
  approvedForFTax: boolean;
  updatedAt?: string;
};

export const DEFAULT_BRANDING: Branding = {
  companyName: "",
  logoPath: null,
  logoUrl: null,
  streetAddress: "",
  postalCode: "",
  city: "",
  country: "",
  phone: "",
  email: "",
  organizationNumber: "",
  vatNumber: "",
  website: "",
  certificationText: "",
  certificationNumber: "",
  invoiceDetails: "",
  approvedForFTax: false,
};

const BRANDING_CACHE_KEY = "lager-branding";

export function normalizeBranding(value: Partial<Branding> | null | undefined): Branding {
  const textFields = [
    "companyName", "streetAddress", "postalCode", "city", "country", "phone", "email",
    "organizationNumber", "vatNumber", "website", "certificationText",
    "certificationNumber", "invoiceDetails",
  ] as const;
  const normalized = { ...DEFAULT_BRANDING };
  for (const field of textFields) {
    normalized[field] = typeof value?.[field] === "string" ? value[field] : "";
  }
  normalized.logoPath = typeof value?.logoPath === "string" ? value.logoPath : null;
  normalized.logoUrl = typeof value?.logoUrl === "string" ? value.logoUrl : null;
  normalized.updatedAt = typeof value?.updatedAt === "string" ? value.updatedAt : undefined;
  normalized.approvedForFTax = value?.approvedForFTax === true;
  return normalized;
}

function getCachedBranding(): Branding {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return DEFAULT_BRANDING;

  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem(BRANDING_CACHE_KEY) ?? "null") as Partial<Branding> | null;
    return normalizeBranding(parsed);
  } catch {
    return DEFAULT_BRANDING;
  }
}

const BrandingContext = createContext<Branding>(DEFAULT_BRANDING);

export function BrandingProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const { data } = useQuery<Branding>({
    queryKey: ["/api/branding"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const result = await customFetch<Partial<Branding>>("/api/branding");
      const next = normalizeBranding(result);
      try {
        window.localStorage.setItem(BRANDING_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(next));
      } catch {
        // localStorage may be unavailable in private browsing; the API remains the source of truth.
      }
      return next;
    },
    placeholderData: getCachedBranding(),
    staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000,
  });

  const branding = data ?? DEFAULT_BRANDING;

  useEffect(() => {
    const version = encodeURIComponent(branding.updatedAt ?? "2");
    const basePath = import.meta.env.BASE_URL.replace(/\/$/, "");
    const iconUrl = (size: number) => `${basePath}/api/branding/icon?size=${size}&v=${version}`;
    const manifestUrl = `${basePath}/api/branding/manifest.webmanifest?v=${version}`;

    document.title = branding.companyName || "Lagersystem";
    document.getElementById("app-favicon")?.setAttribute("href", iconUrl(192));
    document.getElementById("app-apple-touch-icon")?.setAttribute("href", iconUrl(180));
    document.getElementById("app-manifest")?.setAttribute("href", manifestUrl);
  }, [branding.companyName, branding.updatedAt]);

  return (
    <BrandingContext.Provider value={branding}>
      {children}
    </BrandingContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useBranding() {
  return useContext(BrandingContext);
}

import { useEffect } from "react";
  import { SidebarProvider, SidebarTrigger } from "@/components/ui/sidebar";
  import { Sidebar } from "./Sidebar";
import logoVirke from "@/assets/logo-virke.png";
import { useBranding } from "@/components/branding/BrandingContext";

  interface LayoutProps {
    children: React.ReactNode;
  }

  export function Layout({ children }: LayoutProps) {
    const branding = useBranding();
    useEffect(() => {
      const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
        if (e.key === "F12") {
          e.preventDefault();
          window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("shortcut:print"));
        }
      };
      window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
      return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    }, []);

    return (
      <SidebarProvider>
        <div className="flex min-h-screen w-full bg-background">
          <Sidebar />
          <main className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden min-w-0">
            {/* z-20 + relative: håller headern över sidans eget innehåll (t.ex.
                sticky page-headers som redan använder z-10, se
                InventeringSkanningPage.tsx). paddingTop lägger själva
                statusfälts-/Dynamic Island-utrymmet OVANPÅ den vanliga py-2,
                så knappen och loggan alltid hamnar tydligt under det —
                krävs eftersom viewport-fit=cover (index.html, för kamerans
                helskärmsvy) gör att safe-area-insets annars är 0 och appen
                ritas rakt under statusfältet i Safari/PWA-standalone-läge. */}
            <header
              className="relative z-20 flex items-center gap-2 border-b px-4 py-2 md:hidden bg-card shrink-0"
              style={{ paddingTop: "calc(env(safe-area-inset-top) + 0.5rem)" }}
            >
              <SidebarTrigger />
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <img src={branding.logoUrl ?? logoVirke} alt={branding.companyName} className="h-8 w-8 object-contain" />
                <span className="font-semibold text-sm">{branding.companyName}</span>
              </div>
            </header>
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-auto p-4 md:p-8">
              {children}
            </div>
          </main>
        </div>
      </SidebarProvider>
    );
  }
